### 1. SoAd

#### 基本信息

- **UUID**: `4231da07-9bd4-4fb6-8568-cea2233c2c32`
- **模块名称**: `SoAd`
- **类别**: `VENDOR_SPECIFIC_MODULE_DEFINITION`

- **支持的变体**:

#### 模块描述

The Socket Adaptor is an interface between an AUTOSAR communication service module using PDUs and a socket based TCP/IP stack.
#Ethernet #SomeIpSd
#AUTOSAR

#### 配置容器

本模块包含 **31** 个配置容器：

##### 1. SoAdBswModules

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `e38bf46e-bcae-4c5a-87d4-3c5269ce8d0d`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `SoAdBswModules`
- **多重性**: [0..infinite]
- **需要索引**: false
- **多配置容器**: false

**容器描述**:

Each container describes a specific BSW module that the SoAd shall interface to.

###### 参数配置

此容器包含 **12** 个参数：

####### BOOLEAN 类型参数

######## 1. SoAdIf

- **UUID**: `3a92407d-bf4e-4cf0-a911-5335ab35f70f`
- **参数名**: `SoAdIf`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter defines if the BSW module supports the Communication Interface APIs or not. Value true means that the APIs are supported. A module can have both Communication Interface APIs and Transport Protocol APIs (e.g. the PduR module).

######## 2. SoAdIfTriggerTransmit

- **UUID**: `fd1f5968-5e27-4bab-a472-3b9e5453f852`
- **参数名**: `SoAdIfTriggerTransmit`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter defines if the BSW module supports the TriggerTransmit API or not. Value true means that the API is supported.

######## 3. SoAdIfTxConfirmation

- **UUID**: `4e2b064d-1665-4f8f-bea0-6f8546424bdd`
- **参数名**: `SoAdIfTxConfirmation`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter defines if the BSW module supports the TxConfirmation API or not. Value true means that the API is supported.

######## 4. SoAdLocalIpAddrAssigmentChg

- **UUID**: `e3b2a69d-c684-4fd4-8cc1-b9579289b67c`
- **参数名**: `SoAdLocalIpAddrAssigmentChg`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter defines if the BSW module supports the LocalIpAddrAssigmentChg API or not. Value true means that the API is supported.

######## 5. SoAdSoConModeChg

- **UUID**: `add80472-7f4a-4ced-af43-f25280b8fd29`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSoConModeChg`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter defines if the BSW module supports the SoConModeChg API or not. Value true means that the API is supported.

######## 6. SoAdTp

- **UUID**: `ba22283e-14e3-47a5-9937-b1edd99b28f6`
- **参数名**: `SoAdTp`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter defines if the BSW module supports the TransportProtocol APIs or not. Value true means that the APIs are supported. A module can have both Communication Interface APIs and Transport Protocol APIs (e.g. the PduR module).

######## 7. SoAdUseCallerInfix

- **UUID**: `32151598-c92a-4b21-bee8-0a274e9d657c`
- **参数名**: `SoAdUseCallerInfix`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `true`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter defines if SoAd shall use (TRUE) the infix "SoAd" when calling an upper layer module function or not (FALSE). E.g. if SoAdUseCallerInfix is TRUE for the upper layer "ABC" then SoAd will call ABC_SoAdIfRxIndication() otherwise SoAd would call ABC_IfRxIndication().

######## 8. SoAdUseTypeInfix

- **UUID**: `e0f2ae36-c1de-4f2e-b382-753391471792`
- **参数名**: `SoAdUseTypeInfix`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `true`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter defines if SoAd shall use (TRUE) the API type infix "Tp" or "If" when calling an upper layer module function or not (FALSE). E.g. if SoAdUseTypeInfix is TRUE for the upper layer "ABC" then SoAd will call ABC_IfRxIndication(), otherwise SoAd would call ABC_RxIndication().

######## 9. SoAdShutdownFinishedCbk

- **UUID**: `9d8b6f7a-471e-40f5-8964-507cc2e73a68`
- **参数名**: `SoAdShutdownFinishedCbk`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter enables a callback which is called if SoAd is in shutdown state after successful call to SoAd_Shutdown().

######## 10. SoAdTpCopyTxDataWithConstPointer

- **UUID**: `f4cb85d7-effb-4b49-87d6-5f0235e20fd7`
- **参数名**: `SoAdTpCopyTxDataWithConstPointer`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter specifies if the upper layer Ul_[SoAd][Tp]CopyTxData() function is implemented with constant pointer for parameter "info".

######## 11. SoAdTpCopyRxDataWithConstPointer

- **UUID**: `46c95bce-d6e9-42cf-86d7-fa0f533aa6ed`

###### 引用关系

此容器包含 **1** 个引用：

####### FOREIGN-REFERENCE 类型引用

######## 1. SoAdBswModuleRef

- **UUID**: `95ed6f88-108a-4626-9441-fb4333d9b903`
- **引用名**: `SoAdBswModuleRef`
- **引用类型**: FOREIGN-REFERENCE
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **目标类型**: ECUC-MODULE-CONFIGURATION-VALUES
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: Reference to one BSW module's configuration (i.e. not the ECUC parameter definition template).
Example, there could be several configurations of PduR and this reference selects one of them.

SoAd has to figure out from the structure of the referenced BSW module's configuration, what kind of upper layer it deals with.
In case of a CDD SoAd expects UL-APIs in form of _SoAd<If|Tp><function> and expects CDD Pdu configuration structures according to the Ecu Configuration specification (chapter CDD module\Socket Adaptor).
In case it is one of the standardized AUTOSAR BSW modules, the configuration structures and API names for interaction with SoAd are defined in the corresponding SWS.

##### 2. SoAdConfig

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `1184255e-b46f-420e-b4bc-cc7ed0c1c11f`

- **容器名称**: `SoAdConfig`
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **需要索引**: false
- **多配置容器**: false

**容器描述**:

This container contains the configuration parameters and sub containers of the AUTOSAR SoAd module. This container is a MultipleConfigurationContainer, i.e. this container and its sub-containers exist once per configuration set.

###### 配置容器

本模块包含 **20** 个配置容器：

####### 1. SoAdPduRoute

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `c32ed2df-5355-4060-bdef-7b09670d11e4`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `SoAdPduRoute`
- **多重性**: [0..infinite]
- **需要索引**: false
- **多配置容器**: false

**容器描述**:

Describes the path of a PDU from an upper layer of the SoAd to the socket in the TCP/IP stack for transmission.

######## 参数配置

此容器包含 **8** 个参数：

######### INTEGER 类型参数

########## 1. SoAdTxPduId

- **UUID**: `309a330c-09f0-44f9-bccd-6f89a78b1796`
- **参数名**: `SoAdTxPduId`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `0`
- **取值范围**: [0..65535]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: true
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This value specifies the Tx PDU ID of the PDU coming from the PDU Router.

######### BOOLEAN 类型参数

########## 1. SoAdTxIfTriggerTransmit

- **UUID**: `9a5e32ea-9a84-46a5-a032-e7f7c204b738`
- **参数名**: `SoAdTxIfTriggerTransmit`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter defines if this PDU is transmitted via TriggerTransmit API.
true: On transmission current value is retrieved via call to TriggerTransmit API of upper layer.
false: Transmission is performed in context of transmit service.

In context of nPduUdpTxBuffer this behavior can be used to optimize the nPduUdpTxBuffer and replace it by a request queue.

########## 2. SoAdTxTpOptimized

- **UUID**: `95e35cc7-1656-477c-962f-b3c07c1b5895`
- **参数名**: `SoAdTxTpOptimized`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter defines if optimization for TP transmission shall be used for this PDU.
true: SoAd handles transmission in context of SoAd_TpTransmit i.e. TpCopyTxData and TpTxConfirmation of upper layer may be called in context of SoAd_TpTransmit.
false: SoAd stores transmission request in SoAd_TpTransmit and handles transmission in SoAd_MainFunction (accroding to AUTOSAR).

########## 3. SoAdTxIfOptimized

- **UUID**: `085f4e65-272c-4d17-8f50-7fbce413cdfd`
- **参数名**: `SoAdTxIfOptimized`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter defines if optimization for IF transmission shall be used for this PDU.
true: SoAd handles the transmission in context of SoAd_IfTransmit. This means Ul_[SoAd][If]TxConfirmation and Ul_[SoAd][If]TriggerTransmit of upper layer may be called in context of SoAd_IfTransmit.
false: SoAd stores and forwards transmission requests according to AUTOSAR

########## 4. SoAdTxIfTriggerTransmitForceSingleCall

- **UUID**: `80ce2ab9-3960-442d-8c4a-401300b4e945`
- **参数名**: `SoAdTxIfTriggerTransmitForceSingleCall`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter defines if the Ul_[SoAd][If]TriggerTransmit API is forced to be called only once. In case of a PDU fan-out, SoAd calls Ul_[SoAd][If]TriggerTransmit for each SoAdSocketConnection, otherwise. If this parameter is enabled, a SoAdIfTriggerTransmitBuffer is used to store the PDU data retrieved by Ul_[SoAd][If]TriggerTransmit before initiating the PDU fan-out.
true: SoAd calls Ul_[SoAd][If]TriggerTransmit only once.
false: SoAd calls Ul_[SoAd][If]TriggerTransmit for each SoAdSocketConnection.

########## 5. SoAdTxIfOptimisticTransmit

- **UUID**: `fe82b657-77f7-4341-b987-38c51380c538`
- **参数名**: `SoAdTxIfOptimisticTransmit`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter defines if the return value of an IF transmission procedure shall be handled optimistic in case of transmission fan-out on more than one PduRouteDest.
true: Transmission is handled as successful (SoAd_IfTransmit returns E_OK) in case transmission succeeded at least for one PduRouteDest of the PduRoute.
false: Transmission is only handled as successful in case transmission succeeded for all PduRouteDest of the PduRoute (standard AUTOSAR behavior).

######### ENUMERATION 类型参数

########## 1. SoAdTxUpperLayerType

- **UUID**: `7f0c87ef-e801-4625-b9a5-3c25a1e726d6`
- **参数名**: `SoAdTxUpperLayerType`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `IF`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `IF` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
  - `TP` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
- **描述**: This paramater defines the upper layer interface type (must be "IF" in case of multiple PduRoutes).

########## 2. SoAdTxPduCollectionSemantics

- **UUID**: `174f5ac2-193b-4e1c-b38a-6e996c18d3f2`
- **参数名**: `SoAdTxPduCollectionSemantics`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `SOAD_COLLECT_QUEUED`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `SOAD_COLLECT_LAST_IS_BEST` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
  - `SOAD_COLLECT_QUEUED` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
- **描述**: This parameter defines if this PDU shall be collected using a queued or last-is-best semantics. This parameter is only relevant if the PDU collection feature is enabled.
This parameter depends on SoAdTxIfTriggerTransmit.
For SOAD_COLLECT_LAST_IS_BEST set SoAdTxIfTriggerTransmit to true.
For SOAD_COLLECT_QUEUED set SoAdTxIfTriggerTransmit to false.

######### 1. SoAdPduRouteDest

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `227a3664-04ef-4b56-b368-68313d1a7c33`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `SoAdPduRouteDest`
- **多重性**: [1..infinite]
- **需要索引**: false
- **多配置容器**: false

**容器描述**:

Specifies the PDU route destination.

########## 参数配置

此容器包含 **3** 个参数：

########### INTEGER 类型参数

############ 1. SoAdTxPduHeaderId

- **UUID**: `77d256f2-52ae-4af6-ae7a-4710b7769f6f`
- **参数名**: `SoAdTxPduHeaderId`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `0`
- **取值范围**: [0..4294967296]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This value specifies the ID to be sent on the TCP/IP connection if the PDU header option is enabled.

########### FLOAT 类型参数

############ 1. SoAdTxUdpTriggerTimeout

- **UUID**: `ed4eaee5-16cd-4499-9f41-a4c07fae0cf6`
- **参数名**: `SoAdTxUdpTriggerTimeout`
- **数据类型**: FLOAT
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `2`
- **取值范围**: [0..65535]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This value specifies the timeout in [s] the nPduUdpTxBuffer shall be transmitted at the latest after this PDU is put into the buffer. This optional parameter is only relevant if SoAdTxUdpTriggerMode is TRIGGER_NEVER.

Note: In case the timeout is configured to a value smaller than the SoAdMainFunctionPeriod the timeout is set to one main funtion cycle.

########### ENUMERATION 类型参数

############ 1. SoAdTxUdpTriggerMode

- **UUID**: `348cdb69-8b4e-46cf-b576-8e03b6195e3f`
- **参数名**: `SoAdTxUdpTriggerMode`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `TRIGGER_ALWAYS`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `TRIGGER_ALWAYS` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
  - `TRIGGER_NEVER` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
- **描述**: This parameter defines whether a PDU triggers the transmission of the nPduUdpTxBuffer. NPduUdpTxBuffer can only be used for upper layers with "IF" API, i.e. this parameter shall only be set if all upper layers belonging to the related socket connection have SoAdTxUpperLayerType set to "IF".
This parameter is only relevant for UDP connections.

TRIGGER_ALWAYS: PDU transmission triggers transmission of nPduUdpTxBuffer
TRIGGER_NEVER: PDU transmission is stored in nPduUdpTxBuffer and triggers nPduUdpTxBuffer transmission only if buffer size is not sufficient

Note: nPduUdpTxBuffer is not required in case TRIGGER_ALWAYS is used on all related SoAdPduRouteDest.

####### 3. SoAdRoutingGroup

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `88f63feb-72a8-400b-818b-2c686daae84e`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `SoAdRoutingGroup`
- **多重性**: [0..infinite]
- **需要索引**: false
- **多配置容器**: false

**容器描述**:

Each container describes a specific routing group which can be enabled or disabled. A routing group consists of PDUs. Routing of PDUs can either be forwarding of PDUs from the upper layer to a TCP or UDP socket of the TCP/IP stack specified by a SoAdPduRoute or the other way around specified by a SoAdSocketRoute.

######## 参数配置

此容器包含 **3** 个参数：

######### INTEGER 类型参数

########## 1. SoAdRoutingGroupId

- **UUID**: `c8757cea-4c2f-49f9-8b89-e4a43a07a1f2`
- **参数名**: `SoAdRoutingGroupId`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `0`
- **取值范围**: [0..65535]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: true
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This value specifies a unique ID of Routing Group

######### BOOLEAN 类型参数

########## 1. SoAdRoutingGroupIsEnabledAtInit

- **UUID**: `1e212995-c561-4b62-a174-6ea8f2a83669`
- **参数名**: `SoAdRoutingGroupIsEnabledAtInit`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `true`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter defines if this routing group is enabled (TRUE) after initializing the SoAd module (i.e. enabled in the SoAd_Init function) or not (FALSE).

########## 2. SoAdRoutingGroupTxTriggerable

- **UUID**: `d494337b-9309-4d2d-93fb-13707aa7192c`
- **参数名**: `SoAdRoutingGroupTxTriggerable`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter defines if the IF Tx-PDUs related to the PduRouteDest containers referenced by this routing group can be triggered via SoAd_IfRoutingGroupTransmit (TRUE) or not (FALSE).

####### 4. SoAdSocketConnectionGroup

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `88c5e842-9c21-4a26-a037-306f08c46e6b`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `SoAdSocketConnectionGroup`
- **多重性**: [1..infinite]
- **需要索引**: false
- **多配置容器**: false

**容器描述**:

Specifies the configuration of a socket connection group, i.e. specifies the socket connections belonging to the group and the parameters which are common for all socket connections of the group. A socket connection specifies how data can be received and transmited via a TCP or UDP socket.

######## 参数配置

此容器包含 **15** 个参数：

######### INTEGER 类型参数

########## 1. SoAdSocketFramePriority

- **UUID**: `0a8e75aa-98c4-4022-bbdb-d1247a0bb3bf`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSocketFramePriority`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `7`
- **取值范围**: [0..7]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This value specifies the priority of the Ethernet frame.
If IEEE 802.1Q VLAN Tags are used, the specified priority will be used in the VLAN Tag PCP field. If this optional parameter is not available the default priority specified in the TcpIp module is used.

########## 2. SoAdSocketLocalPort

- **UUID**: `10fa2471-0d25-45de-837c-dc4cf8515064`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSocketLocalPort`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `0`
- **取值范围**: [0..65535]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This value specifies the local UDP or TCP port used for this connection. If this parameter is set to 0 SoAd requests TcpIp to select an ephemeral port.

########## 3. SoAdSocketTpRxBufferMin

- **UUID**: `26bec412-742f-49e3-b833-515dcdd7ad14`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSocketTpRxBufferMin`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `512`
- **取值范围**: [0..65535]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This value specifies the amount of data in bytes (PDU data for the upper layer and PDU Header if used) the SoAd shall at least be able to buffer for data reception via each socket connection of the socket connection group and using an upper layer with TP.
Note: in case of a TCP socket where PduHeaderMode is used and an upper layer with IF-API, the required buffer size can be determined automatically.

########## 4. SoAdSocketDifferentiatedServicesField

- **UUID**: `b129864f-33e6-42c3-a627-3e7a30ca6bad`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSocketDifferentiatedServicesField`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `0`
- **取值范围**: [0..63]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This value specifies the 6-bit Differentiated Service Field in the IP headers which may be used for classifying network traffic.

This parameter is not yet supported and its value is not evaluated but the parameter exists for compatibility reasons.

########## 5. SoAdSocketFlowLabel

- **UUID**: `be543221-99b3-4672-97d9-277b2cd03c66`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSocketFlowLabel`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `0`
- **取值范围**: [0..1048575]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This value specifies the 20-bit Flow Label field in the IPv6 header which may be used by a source to label sequences of packets for which it requests special handling by the IPv6 routers, such as non-default quality of service.

This parameter is not yet supported and its value is not evaluated but the parameter exists for compatibility reasons.

######### BOOLEAN 类型参数

########## 1. SoAdPduHeaderEnable

- **UUID**: `5fa007e2-b392-4300-9ad9-5d3192f2eec4`
- **参数名**: `SoAdPduHeaderEnable`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter defines the usage of the PDU header (ID, length) on all socket connections of this group.
TRUE: Add SoAd PDU header before PDU data.
FALSE: No SoAd PDU header is used.

########## 2. SoAdSocketAutomaticSoConSetup

- **UUID**: `49e18d91-1bb4-4344-a0f1-3498a69c9084`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSocketAutomaticSoConSetup`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `true`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter defines if the setup of the socket connection shall be done automatically (TRUE) or manually (FALSE) via SoAd_OpenSoCon() and SoAd_CloseSoCon().

########## 3. SoAdSocketIpAddrAssignmentChgNotification

- **UUID**: `97182fe0-9616-45ea-8c5c-01c2c95f0c3b`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSocketIpAddrAssignmentChgNotification`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter defines if the local IP address assignment change notification callback function of the upper layer shall be called if the assignment of the local IP address used by this socket connection changes.

########## 4. SoAdSocketMsgAcceptanceFilterEnabled

- **UUID**: `eaf1dfea-0497-4c25-8f39-fd1e0b88f6cb`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSocketMsgAcceptanceFilterEnabled`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `true`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter defines if the message acceptance filter is enabled (TRUE) or not (FALSE).
Note: if a wildcard is used in SoAdSocketRemoteAddress AND SoAdSocketUdpListenOnly is FALSE, this parameter must be TRUE.
Note: if multiple SoAdSocketConnections are configured for one SoAdSocketConnectionGroup, this parameter must be TRUE.

########## 5. SoAdSocketSoConModeChgNotification

- **UUID**: `31bbf9a1-d895-4900-b50f-80fcb5aec4b5`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSocketSoConModeChgNotification`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter defines if the SoCon mode change notification callback function of the upper layer shall be called in case of SoCon mode change.

########## 6. SoAdBestMatchWithSockRouteEnabled

- **UUID**: `f82012a4-1978-4f87-9c45-62403f788a84`
- **参数名**: `SoAdBestMatchWithSockRouteEnabled`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter defines if the best match algorithm for this socket connection group considers that a socket route is configured for a socket connection.
This means: The best match algortihm will not choose a socket connection which has no receiver even if the remote address matches in case this parameter is enabled.

########## 7. SoAdBestMatchWithPduHeaderEnabled

- **UUID**: `3ce92643-63cb-4ebe-9880-b104ead0de5f`
- **参数名**: `SoAdBestMatchWithPduHeaderEnabled`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter defines if the best match algorithm of this socket connection group considers the received PDU Header when choosing a socket connection. A received PDU is discarded if no socket route could be found containing the corresponding PDU Header ID in case this parameter is enabled.

########## 8. SoAdSocketAutomaticSoConSetupKeepOnline

- **UUID**: `9d4a5f5c-2137-4c89-a2d8-a51c12efeead`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSocketAutomaticSoConSetupKeepOnline`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter defines if the socket connection is kept online on transmission in case automatic socket connection setup is used together with a remote address containing wildcards.
According to AUTOSAR, a socket connection can be opened on UDP reception or on TCP connection establishment. This applies if the configured remote address contains wildcards. After transmission of a PDU, the socket connection shall be closed.

TRUE: The socket connection is not closed on PDU transmission.
FALSE: The socket connection is closed on PDU transmission (standard AUTOSAR behavior).

########## 9. SoAdSocketPathMTUEnable

- **UUID**: `d64cd50b-db59-4d21-9831-6b83212d1990`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSocketPathMTUEnable`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter defines if path MTU discovery shall be performed for this connection.

This parameter is not yet supported and its value is not evaluated but the parameter exists for compatibility reasons.

######### ENUMERATION 类型参数

########## 1. SoAdMeasurementDataSomeIpSdType

- **UUID**: `c81d2f1e-4ff5-4041-b814-9e3b7d60b3ec`
- **参数名**: `SoAdMeasurementDataSomeIpSdType`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `NONE` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `SOME_IP` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `SOME_IP_SD` (来源: Vector Informatik)
- **描述**: This parameter enables/disables the SOME/IP and SD measurement data counting invalid service IDs and method IDs inside the PDU header for all socket routes of the socket connection group. This is only possible if SoAdGetAndResetMeasurementDataSomeIpSd == TRUE.

NONE: Counting of invalid IDs is not enabled for this socket connection group.
SOME_IP: Invalid service and method IDs are counted into the SOME/IP counters.
SOME_IP_SD: Invalid service and method IDs are counted into the SOME/IP SD counters.

######## 配置容器

本模块包含 **5** 个配置容器：

######### 1. SoAdSocketConnection

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `7d77d0d1-ef5e-4898-81ee-7296f9474dd9`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `SoAdSocketConnection`
- **多重性**: [1..infinite]
- **需要索引**: false
- **多配置容器**: false

**容器描述**:

Specifies the socket connection (Id and remote address information).
Note: Parameters which are common to all socket connections of a socket connection group are specified directly on the group.

########## 参数配置

此容器包含 **4** 个参数：

########### INTEGER 类型参数

############ 1. SoAdSocketId

- **UUID**: `846b9a8d-11d9-4e1d-9404-5654dc6f2e74`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSocketId`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `0`
- **取值范围**: [0..65535]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: true
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This value specifies the unique identifier of the socket connection.

############ 2. SoAdTcpTxQueueSize

- **UUID**: `3fc39180-29b7-48d8-979c-9ff1da354c6a`
- **参数名**: `SoAdTcpTxQueueSize`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `1`
- **取值范围**: [1..65535]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter defines the size of a TxQueue to support parallel transmission requests on TCP and a streaming-based TxConfirmation.

########### BOOLEAN 类型参数

############ 1. SoAdSocketGetReceivedRemoteAddressEnabled

- **UUID**: `9ae969e9-225d-4a3e-b614-9c36f434853d`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSocketGetReceivedRemoteAddressEnabled`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter defines the ability to store the remote IP address of the last received data on the corresponding Socket Connection.

The remote IP address can be retrived by the upper layer by calling the SoAd_GetRcvRemoteAddr() API with the corresponding Socket Connection ID as function parameter.

false:    Remote IP address of last received data on Socket Connection isn't stored
true:     Remote IP address of last received data on Socket Connection is stored and can be retrieved by calling the SoAd_GetRcvRemoteAddr() API

############ 2. SoAdVerifyRxPduEnable

- **UUID**: `bec3febc-35c5-40a8-97ff-b5eb837dbe9b`
- **参数名**: `SoAdVerifyRxPduEnable`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter enables the usage of the Rx PDU verification callback on this socket connection.
The corresponding callback is configured by parameter SoAdVerifyRxPduCallback.

########## 引用关系

此容器包含 **3** 个引用：

########### REFERENCE 类型引用

############ 1. SoAdAdditionalChgNotificationRef

- **UUID**: `b55c6185-35e6-45c1-beca-9c52edaa0a0f`

Which notification is called depends on specific SoAdBswModule configuration (parameter SoAdLocalIpAddrAssigmentChg and SoAdSoConModeChg).
SoAdBswModules which are already registered via SoAdPduRouteDest or SoAdSocketRoute must not be added here.

- **描述**: Reference to an additional callback to notify any module about a socket connection state change on this socket connection.

- **描述**: Reference to an additional callback to notify any module about a local IP address assignment change on this socket connection.

########## 配置容器

本模块包含 **1** 个配置容器：

########### 1. SoAdSocketRemoteAddress

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `28b40b24-0258-48a3-a936-7c1a76e253f2`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `SoAdSocketRemoteAddress`
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **需要索引**: false
- **多配置容器**: false

**容器描述**:

Subcontainer of SoAdSocketConnection to specify the remote address (IP address and port) for a socket connection.
If remote address contains wildcards socket connection cannot be opened (set to online). The remote address has to be overwritten at runtime via API call, on UDP frame reception or via TCP connection establishment.
If SoAdSocketRemoteAddress is not specified the remote address has to be set by the upper layer via SoAd_SetRemoteAddr().

############ 参数配置

此容器包含 **4** 个参数：

############# INTEGER 类型参数

############## 1. SoAdSocketRemotePort

- **UUID**: `af06bc0c-39be-483d-9355-d635cf012b3a`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSocketRemotePort`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `0`
- **取值范围**: [0..65535]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This value specifies the UDP or TCP port of remote node.
To accept any remote port, set SoAdSocketRemotePort to 0. See message acceptance policy for more details.
If this parameter is set to 0 socket connection cannot be openned (set to online). The remote address has to be overwritten at runtime via API call, on UDP frame reception or via TCP connection establishment.

############# BOOLEAN 类型参数

############## 1. SoAdSocketRemoteIpAddressNotSet

- **UUID**: `015c49f3-23fb-4c5c-992a-a63f5ccdbe78`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSocketRemoteIpAddressNotSet`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter defines whether remote IP address is set (valid or wildcard) or not set.

True: IP address not set,
False: IP address set (valid/wildcard).

############## 2. SoAdSocketRemotePortNotSet

- **UUID**: `edde5477-e4e5-4eab-b62c-bc3adac91560`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSocketRemotePortNotSet`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter defines whether remote port is set (valid or wildcard) or not set.

True: port not set,
False: port set (valid/wildcard).

############# STRING 类型参数

############## 1. SoAdSocketRemoteIpAddress

- **UUID**: `12785fe4-4bc8-4923-bc2d-498de62320d6`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSocketRemoteIpAddress`
- **数据类型**: STRING
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter defines the IP address of remote node.
To accept any remote IP address, leave this parameter empty. See message acceptance policy for more details.
If this parameter is empty (wildcard) socket connection cannot be openned (set to online). The remote address has to be overwritten at runtime via API call, on UDP frame reception or via TCP connection establishment.

- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `512`
- **取值范围**: [1..65535]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This value specifies a minimum transmission buffer size the TcpIp must be able to provide on each socket connection of this group. The value is forwarded to TcpIp on socket creation at runtime. If this optional parameter is not configured TcpIp will use a default value.

########### BOOLEAN 类型参数

############ 1. SoAdSocketTcpImmediateTpTxConfirmation

- **UUID**: `33fcf06a-e3e5-4a98-a79a-e4715844ce4d`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSocketTcpImmediateTpTxConfirmation`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter defines when SoAd notifies the TP upper layer via transmit confirmation. If set to FALSE, SoAd notifies the TP upper layer via transmit confirmation after a Tcp Ack has been received. If set to TRUE, SoAd notifies the TP upper layer via transmit confirmation immediately after transmit has been accepted by TcpIp.

############ 2. SoAdSocketTcpInitiate

- **UUID**: `a9f6d4f6-c2d0-49fa-aa6c-10c7ccb0abda`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSocketTcpInitiate`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter defines the initiator for this TCP connection. It will not be defined for UDP sockets.
TRUE: This TCP connection is initiated by this module.  
FALSE: This TCP connection is to be initiated in the listen mode.

############ 3. SoAdSocketTcpKeepAlive

- **UUID**: `df38486b-4a1d-4e45-a3ef-02e53bd396ce`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSocketTcpKeepAlive`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter defines if the keep-alive mechanism is used for this connection. It will not be defined for UDP sockets. 
TRUE:      This TCP connection will use the keep-alive mechanism. 
FALSE:     This TCP connection will not use the keep-alive mechanism. 

Note: This parameter must not be set to TRUE if TcpIpTcpKeepAliveEnabled is set to 
FALSE.

############ 4. SoAdSocketTcpNoDelay

- **UUID**: `4ff7b491-2d21-4342-9590-bb020e8b81fd`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSocketTcpNoDelay`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter defines if the congestion control mechanism for this connection is used. It will not be defined for UDP sockets.
TRUE: This TCP connection will NOT use congestion control.
FALSE: This TCP connection will use congestion control.  
If the optional parameter is not enabled, the default behavior configured for TcpIp via the parameter TcpIpTcpNagleEnabled is applied.
Note: This parameter must not be set to FALSE if TcpIpTcpNagleEnabled is set to FALSE.

########### FLOAT 类型参数

############ 1. SoAdSocketTcpKeepAliveInterval

- **UUID**: `bc73148d-b959-4110-8cb3-ed1b8e730bea`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSocketTcpKeepAliveInterval`
- **数据类型**: FLOAT
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `1`
- **取值范围**: [0..4294967]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This value specifies the interval in seconds between subsequent keepalive probes.

Note: The configured value is forwarded to the TcpIp as integer value in seconds. If the value is configured with a higher accuracy, the additional digits are truncated.

############ 2. SoAdSocketTcpKeepAliveTime

- **UUID**: `820713e2-429f-417c-a9ac-652d87ca66f1`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSocketTcpKeepAliveTime`
- **数据类型**: FLOAT
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `7200`
- **取值范围**: [0..4294967]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This value specifies the time in seconds between the last data packet sent and the first keepalive probe.

Note: The configured value is forwarded to the TcpIp as integer value in seconds. If the value is configured with a higher accuracy, the additional digits are truncated.

############ 3. SoAdSocketTcpMsl

- **UUID**: `ea138004-d981-4b8e-a0c9-5c600b8c51a4`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSocketTcpMsl`
- **数据类型**: FLOAT
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **取值范围**: [0..65535]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This value specifies a socket specific maximum segment lifetime (MSL). The configured value is used instead of TcpIpTcpMsl for the corresponding socket. The SoAd calls TcpIp_ChangeParameter to apply the MSL configuration at runtime.

Note: The configured value is forwarded to the TcpIp as integer value in milliseconds. If the value is configured with a higher accuracy, the additional digits are truncated.

############ 4. SoAdSocketTcpAutoConnectTimeout

- **UUID**: `9bc8d70a-8a69-4fe5-aeb3-85c0de0c74f8`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSocketTcpAutoConnectTimeout`
- **数据类型**: FLOAT
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `0`
- **取值范围**: [0..4294967]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This value specifies the time in seconds how long TCP connection attempts are repeated to reach SOAD_SOCON_ONLINE. This parameter is restricted to socket connection groups which are initiating a TCP connection and are under control of SoAd (SoAdSocketAutomaticSoConSetup and SoAdSocketTcpInitiate are enabled in SoAdSocketConnectionGroup).

Note 1: A value of 0 means that the feature is disabled.
Note 2: This parameter and the related functionality is introduced with ASR R21-11.

########## 引用关系

此容器包含 **2** 个引用：

########### REFERENCE 类型引用

############ 1. SoAdSocketTcpTlsConnectionRef

- **UUID**: `ac8ea004-a919-400d-b84a-d4e63a44fd35`
- **引用名**: `SoAdSocketTcpTlsConnectionRef`

- **描述**: Reference to a TLS connection. If set the TCP socket shall use TLS according to the properties of the TLS connection. The SoAd calls TcpIp_ChangeParameter to apply the TLS connection parameter at runtime.

############ 2. SoAdSocketTCPOptionFilterRef

######### 4. SoAdSocketUdp

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `78d1300d-b663-4bbb-ab36-90f1a2fab335`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `SoAdSocketUdp`
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **需要索引**: false
- **多配置容器**: false

**容器描述**:

Specifies that UDP is used as transport protocol for the socket connection group and parameters only related to UDP socket connections.

########## 参数配置

此容器包含 **12** 个参数：

########### INTEGER 类型参数

############ 1. SoAdSocketnPduUdpTxBufferMin

- **UUID**: `d9d68db9-6563-465e-affa-d4d42a2e5beb`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSocketnPduUdpTxBufferMin`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `32`
- **取值范围**: [0..65535]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This value specifies the amount of data in bytes (PDU data provided by the upper layer and PDU Header if used) the SoAd shall be able to buffer for data transmission via this socket connection in case the UDP message shall be buffered for transmission of multiple PDUs per UDP (nPduUdpTxBuffer).

This parameter must not exist if no nPduUdpTxBuffer is used.
Note: in case of a UDP socket and an upper layer with TP API or an upper layer with IF API with UDP transmit retry (for single PDUs) configured, the required buffer size can be determined automatically.

############ 2. SoAdSocketUdpImmediateIfTxConfirmationListSize

- **UUID**: `92a6c3a3-0c04-43d6-836b-2cb473de4958`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSocketUdpImmediateIfTxConfirmationListSize`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `1`
- **取值范围**: [1..255]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This value specifies the size of a list which is used in the TcpIp module to handle UDP transmissions with TxConfirmation. 
Please refer to TcpIp parameter "TcpIpUdpTxReqListSize" for more details.

This parameter is used if "SoAdSocketUdpImmediateIfTxConfirmation" is enabled.

############ 3. SoAdSocketnPduUdpTxQueueSize

- **UUID**: `e23edd63-9e0d-4cff-99be-595f7877da43`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSocketnPduUdpTxQueueSize`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `2`
- **取值范围**: [1..255]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This value specifies the size of a management queue for each socket connection. This queue is used to manage PDUs in the nPduUdpTxBuffer and PDU transmission requests for trigger transmit. Both transmission types are stored within one queue, so make sure that size is sufficient for all related SoAdPduRoutes.

This parameter must not exist if no related SoAdPduRoute exisits with enabled SoAdTxIfTriggerTransmit.

############ 4. SoAdSocketUdpAddressResolutionRetryQueueLimit

- **UUID**: `6221fe99-556f-4b3d-b9b1-c1e406268e82`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSocketUdpAddressResolutionRetryQueueLimit`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `1`
- **取值范围**: [0..255]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This value specifies the limitation of maximum used UDP transmission retry queue elements for this socket. 
The value is forwarded to the TcpIp module on socket creation.
Example: A transmission over a UDP socket fails in TcpIp module since address resolution (ARP) is not finished yet. This transmission is stored in the retry queue and the transmission request is assumed as successful. If the limitation is set to 1 TcpIp will reject further transmission requests until retry queue element is released.

########### BOOLEAN 类型参数

############ 1. SoAdSocketUdpListenOnly

- **UUID**: `71152800-71f8-4175-86d6-11f9c61564ae`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSocketUdpListenOnly`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter defines if the socket connection group is only used for reception (TRUE) or used for both reception and transmission (FALSE).

############ 2. SoAdSocketUdpStrictHeaderLenCheckEnabled

- **UUID**: `72d25a79-49e9-4444-907d-7b6b8bf85614`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSocketUdpStrictHeaderLenCheckEnabled`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter defines if UDP messages shall be dropped (TRUE) if the length of all contained PDUs does not match the length of the whole message or not (FALSE). Shall only be set to TRUE if SoAdPduHeaderEnable is also set to TRUE.

############ 3. SoAdSocketUdpChecksumEnabled

- **UUID**: `77bab4af-6816-424f-ba2a-5778e2e2dca0`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSocketUdpChecksumEnabled`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `true`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter specifies if UDP checksum calculation shall be enabled (TRUE) or disabled (FALSE) on the related socket. FALSE implies that the upper layer of the socket connection is either capable to handle malformed messages or applies a checksum mechanism itself.

Note:
This parameter is ignored when SoAdSocketUdpChecksumChangeEnabled is disabled.

When the parameter is set to FALSE the following applies:
 Rx: Checksum of 0 is accepted and the frame is forwarded. Checksums unequal to 0 will be checked.
 Tx: Checksum is not calculated and the value is set to 0.

############ 4. SoAdSocketUdpAddressResolutionRetryEnabled

- **UUID**: `8d4c918f-c16f-45da-a583-336c1843ed67`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSocketUdpAddressResolutionRetryEnabled`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `true`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter defines if a transmission request shall be stored in TcpIp module to retry transmission in case of missing address resolution (ARP/NDP).
True: Retry enabled
False: Retry disabled

############ 5. SoAdSocketUdpImmediateIfTxConfirmation

- **UUID**: `0d7bf58c-c61b-484c-ab23-2011df725c2c`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSocketUdpImmediateIfTxConfirmation`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter defines the ability to notify the IF upper layers via TX Confirmation in context of Ethernet driver's TX Confirmation.

All upper layers using a Socket Connection of a Socket Connection Group configured for UDP communication with this feature enabled will be notified by the TX Confirmation in context of Ethernet driver's TX Confirmation.

false:    Upper Layers are notified by the TX Confirmation called in next main function cycle of SoAd (Task-Context). The call is independent from the TX Confirmation by the lower layer of SoAd.
true:    Upper Layers are notified by the TX Confirmation propagated through the Ethernet Stack starting by the Ethernet Driver (Task- or Interrupt-Context).

############ 6. SoAdSocketUdpChecksumChangeEnabled

- **UUID**: `3c6ecc0f-c3db-467a-bd1e-6698db2394c1`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSocketUdpChecksumChangeEnabled`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter defines if UDP checksum calculation setting shall be changed (TRUE) or not (FALSE) on the related socket. If enabled, the SoAd calls TcpIp_ChangeParameter with the corresponding value of SoAdSocketUdpChecksumEnabled to apply the UDP checksum calculation configuration at runtime.

Note:
This parameter must be disabled when...
...UDP frames are used via IPv6. A checksum is mandatory in this case.
...vBsdAd TcpIp is used (SoAdVBsdAdUsed).

Additionally, when UDP checksum offloading is done in hardware (EthCtrlEnableOffloadChecksumUDP) this parameter is usually expected to be disabled. If you enable it be aware that HW could overwrite checksums of '0' with a valid calculated checksum which can not be detected by SW.

########### FLOAT 类型参数

############ 1. SoAdSocketUdpAliveSupervisionTimeout

- **UUID**: `ee9cea99-da8e-406d-bc2f-724a1f513b16`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSocketUdpAliveSupervisionTimeout`
- **数据类型**: FLOAT
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `1`
- **取值范围**: [0..65535]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This value specifies the time in [s] a UDP socket connection remains in the mode SOAD_SOCON_ONLINE after the latest reception of a frame from the remote peer specified by the remote address. If this optional parameter is not enabled UDP Alive Supervision is deactivated for the related socket connection group.

Note: In case the timeout is configured to a value smaller than the SoAdMainFunctionPeriod the timeout is set to one main funtion cycle.

############ 2. SoAdSocketUdpTriggerTimeout

- **UUID**: `6033cc52-fffa-433b-a5d5-74879ea83812`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSocketUdpTriggerTimeout`
- **数据类型**: FLOAT
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `2`
- **取值范围**: [0..65535]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This value specifies the timeout in [s] a nPduUdpTxBuffer is waiting for a PDU with TriggerMode = TRIGGER_ALWAYS, i.e. when the timeout expires the nPduUdpTxBuffer is transmitted. Timer is reset after each UDP transmission. This optional parameter is only relevant if a nPduUdpTxBuffer is used.

Note: In case the timeout is configured to a value smaller than the SoAdMainFunctionPeriod the timeout is set to one main funtion cycle.

######### 5. SoAdSocketProtocol

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `16b75c1a-d5f9-4eb6-8408-d50cc5051fe0`
- **容器类型**: CHOICE-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `SoAdSocketProtocol`

  - 描述: Specifies that TCP is used as transport protocol for the socket connection group and parameters only related to TCP socket connections.
- **SoAdSocketUdp**
  - UUID: `78d1300d-b663-4bbb-ab36-90f1a2fab335`
  - 描述: Specifies that UDP is used as transport protocol for the socket connection group and parameters only related to UDP socket connections.

**容器描述**:

Specifies the transport protocol and transport protocol specific parameters used for the socket connections of the socket connection group.

####### 5. SoAdSocketConnection

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `7d77d0d1-ef5e-4898-81ee-7296f9474dd9`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `SoAdSocketConnection`
- **多重性**: [1..infinite]
- **需要索引**: false
- **多配置容器**: false

**容器描述**:

Specifies the socket connection (Id and remote address information).
Note: Parameters which are common to all socket connections of a socket connection group are specified directly on the group.

######## 参数配置

此容器包含 **4** 个参数：

######### INTEGER 类型参数

########## 1. SoAdSocketId

- **UUID**: `846b9a8d-11d9-4e1d-9404-5654dc6f2e74`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSocketId`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `0`
- **取值范围**: [0..65535]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: true
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This value specifies the unique identifier of the socket connection.

########## 2. SoAdTcpTxQueueSize

- **UUID**: `3fc39180-29b7-48d8-979c-9ff1da354c6a`
- **参数名**: `SoAdTcpTxQueueSize`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `1`
- **取值范围**: [1..65535]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter defines the size of a TxQueue to support parallel transmission requests on TCP and a streaming-based TxConfirmation.

######### BOOLEAN 类型参数

########## 1. SoAdSocketGetReceivedRemoteAddressEnabled

- **UUID**: `9ae969e9-225d-4a3e-b614-9c36f434853d`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSocketGetReceivedRemoteAddressEnabled`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter defines the ability to store the remote IP address of the last received data on the corresponding Socket Connection.

The remote IP address can be retrived by the upper layer by calling the SoAd_GetRcvRemoteAddr() API with the corresponding Socket Connection ID as function parameter.

false:    Remote IP address of last received data on Socket Connection isn't stored
true:     Remote IP address of last received data on Socket Connection is stored and can be retrieved by calling the SoAd_GetRcvRemoteAddr() API

########## 2. SoAdVerifyRxPduEnable

- **UUID**: `bec3febc-35c5-40a8-97ff-b5eb837dbe9b`
- **参数名**: `SoAdVerifyRxPduEnable`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter enables the usage of the Rx PDU verification callback on this socket connection.
The corresponding callback is configured by parameter SoAdVerifyRxPduCallback.

######## 引用关系

此容器包含 **3** 个引用：

######### REFERENCE 类型引用

########## 1. SoAdAdditionalChgNotificationRef

- **UUID**: `b55c6185-35e6-45c1-beca-9c52edaa0a0f`
- **引用名**: `SoAdAdditionalChgNotificationRef`
- **引用类型**: REFERENCE
- **多重性**: [0..255]
- **目标**: `SoAdBswModules`
- **完整路径**: `/AUTOSAR/EcucDefs/SoAd/SoAdBswModules`

Which notification is called depends on specific SoAdBswModule configuration (parameter SoAdLocalIpAddrAssigmentChg and SoAdSoConModeChg).
SoAdBswModules which are already registered via SoAdPduRouteDest or SoAdSocketRoute must not be added here.

########## 2. SoAdAdditionalSoConModeChgRef

- **UUID**: `94392887-aaff-49f3-b669-2040e794f9ed`

- **引用类型**: REFERENCE
- **多重性**: [0..255]

- **完整路径**: `/MICROSAR/SoAd/SoAdGeneral/SoAdAdditionalSoConModeChgCallback`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: Reference to an additional callback to notify any module about a socket connection state change on this socket connection.

########## 3. SoAdAdditionalLocalIpAddrAssignmentChgRef

- **UUID**: `479cc7d8-b6cf-455d-b9f0-892616923b63`
- **引用名**: `SoAdAdditionalLocalIpAddrAssignmentChgRef`
- **引用类型**: REFERENCE
- **多重性**: [0..255]
- **目标**: `SoAdAdditionalLocalIpAddrAssignmentChgCallback`
- **完整路径**: `/MICROSAR/SoAd/SoAdGeneral/SoAdAdditionalLocalIpAddrAssignmentChgCallback`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: Reference to an additional callback to notify any module about a local IP address assignment change on this socket connection.

######## 配置容器

本模块包含 **1** 个配置容器：

######### 1. SoAdSocketRemoteAddress

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `28b40b24-0258-48a3-a936-7c1a76e253f2`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `SoAdSocketRemoteAddress`
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **需要索引**: false
- **多配置容器**: false

**容器描述**:

Subcontainer of SoAdSocketConnection to specify the remote address (IP address and port) for a socket connection.
If remote address contains wildcards socket connection cannot be opened (set to online). The remote address has to be overwritten at runtime via API call, on UDP frame reception or via TCP connection establishment.
If SoAdSocketRemoteAddress is not specified the remote address has to be set by the upper layer via SoAd_SetRemoteAddr().

########## 参数配置

此容器包含 **4** 个参数：

########### INTEGER 类型参数

############ 1. SoAdSocketRemotePort

- **UUID**: `af06bc0c-39be-483d-9355-d635cf012b3a`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSocketRemotePort`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `0`
- **取值范围**: [0..65535]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This value specifies the UDP or TCP port of remote node.
To accept any remote port, set SoAdSocketRemotePort to 0. See message acceptance policy for more details.
If this parameter is set to 0 socket connection cannot be openned (set to online). The remote address has to be overwritten at runtime via API call, on UDP frame reception or via TCP connection establishment.

########### BOOLEAN 类型参数

############ 1. SoAdSocketRemoteIpAddressNotSet

- **UUID**: `015c49f3-23fb-4c5c-992a-a63f5ccdbe78`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSocketRemoteIpAddressNotSet`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter defines whether remote IP address is set (valid or wildcard) or not set.

True: IP address not set,
False: IP address set (valid/wildcard).

############ 2. SoAdSocketRemotePortNotSet

- **UUID**: `edde5477-e4e5-4eab-b62c-bc3adac91560`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSocketRemotePortNotSet`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter defines whether remote port is set (valid or wildcard) or not set.

True: port not set,
False: port set (valid/wildcard).

########### STRING 类型参数

############ 1. SoAdSocketRemoteIpAddress

- **UUID**: `12785fe4-4bc8-4923-bc2d-498de62320d6`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSocketRemoteIpAddress`
- **数据类型**: STRING
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter defines the IP address of remote node.
To accept any remote IP address, leave this parameter empty. See message acceptance policy for more details.
If this parameter is empty (wildcard) socket connection cannot be openned (set to online). The remote address has to be overwritten at runtime via API call, on UDP frame reception or via TCP connection establishment.

####### 7. SoAdSocketTcp

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `b251c8bd-d43c-4a54-9fed-cc0e7d1e8515`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `SoAdSocketTcp`
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **需要索引**: false
- **多配置容器**: false

**容器描述**:

Specifies that TCP is used as transport protocol for the socket connection group and parameters only related to TCP socket connections.

######## 参数配置

此容器包含 **11** 个参数：

######### INTEGER 类型参数

########## 1. SoAdSocketTcpKeepAliveProbesMax

- **UUID**: `401eceb8-daf0-4be5-a4b2-20eee480dca5`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSocketTcpKeepAliveProbesMax`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `1`
- **取值范围**: [0..65535]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This value specifies the maximum number of times that TCP retransmits an individual data segment before aborting the connection.

########## 2. SoAdSocketTcpTxQuota

- **UUID**: `4392f2a1-0cf8-4a3e-96d5-ad31f7e7d42f`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSocketTcpTxQuota`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `4294967295`
- **取值范围**: [0..4294967295]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter is not applicable and its value is not evaluated but the parameter exists for compatibility reasons.

########## 3. SoAdSocketTcpTxBufferMin

- **UUID**: `de7fc095-e7c1-4d46-8a6d-c8415ef4f066`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSocketTcpTxBufferMin`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `512`
- **取值范围**: [1..65535]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This value specifies a minimum transmission buffer size the TcpIp must be able to provide on each socket connection of this group. The value is forwarded to TcpIp on socket creation at runtime. If this optional parameter is not configured TcpIp will use a default value.

######### BOOLEAN 类型参数

########## 1. SoAdSocketTcpImmediateTpTxConfirmation

- **UUID**: `33fcf06a-e3e5-4a98-a79a-e4715844ce4d`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSocketTcpImmediateTpTxConfirmation`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter defines when SoAd notifies the TP upper layer via transmit confirmation. If set to FALSE, SoAd notifies the TP upper layer via transmit confirmation after a Tcp Ack has been received. If set to TRUE, SoAd notifies the TP upper layer via transmit confirmation immediately after transmit has been accepted by TcpIp.

########## 2. SoAdSocketTcpInitiate

- **UUID**: `a9f6d4f6-c2d0-49fa-aa6c-10c7ccb0abda`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSocketTcpInitiate`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter defines the initiator for this TCP connection. It will not be defined for UDP sockets.
TRUE: This TCP connection is initiated by this module.  
FALSE: This TCP connection is to be initiated in the listen mode.

########## 3. SoAdSocketTcpKeepAlive

- **UUID**: `df38486b-4a1d-4e45-a3ef-02e53bd396ce`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSocketTcpKeepAlive`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter defines if the keep-alive mechanism is used for this connection. It will not be defined for UDP sockets. 
TRUE:      This TCP connection will use the keep-alive mechanism. 
FALSE:     This TCP connection will not use the keep-alive mechanism. 

Note: This parameter must not be set to TRUE if TcpIpTcpKeepAliveEnabled is set to 
FALSE.

########## 4. SoAdSocketTcpNoDelay

- **UUID**: `4ff7b491-2d21-4342-9590-bb020e8b81fd`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSocketTcpNoDelay`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter defines if the congestion control mechanism for this connection is used. It will not be defined for UDP sockets.
TRUE: This TCP connection will NOT use congestion control.
FALSE: This TCP connection will use congestion control.  
If the optional parameter is not enabled, the default behavior configured for TcpIp via the parameter TcpIpTcpNagleEnabled is applied.
Note: This parameter must not be set to FALSE if TcpIpTcpNagleEnabled is set to FALSE.

######### FLOAT 类型参数

########## 1. SoAdSocketTcpKeepAliveInterval

- **UUID**: `bc73148d-b959-4110-8cb3-ed1b8e730bea`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSocketTcpKeepAliveInterval`
- **数据类型**: FLOAT
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `1`
- **取值范围**: [0..4294967]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This value specifies the interval in seconds between subsequent keepalive probes.

Note: The configured value is forwarded to the TcpIp as integer value in seconds. If the value is configured with a higher accuracy, the additional digits are truncated.

########## 2. SoAdSocketTcpKeepAliveTime

- **UUID**: `820713e2-429f-417c-a9ac-652d87ca66f1`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSocketTcpKeepAliveTime`
- **数据类型**: FLOAT
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `7200`
- **取值范围**: [0..4294967]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This value specifies the time in seconds between the last data packet sent and the first keepalive probe.

Note: The configured value is forwarded to the TcpIp as integer value in seconds. If the value is configured with a higher accuracy, the additional digits are truncated.

########## 3. SoAdSocketTcpMsl

- **UUID**: `ea138004-d981-4b8e-a0c9-5c600b8c51a4`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSocketTcpMsl`
- **数据类型**: FLOAT
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **取值范围**: [0..65535]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This value specifies a socket specific maximum segment lifetime (MSL). The configured value is used instead of TcpIpTcpMsl for the corresponding socket. The SoAd calls TcpIp_ChangeParameter to apply the MSL configuration at runtime.

Note: The configured value is forwarded to the TcpIp as integer value in milliseconds. If the value is configured with a higher accuracy, the additional digits are truncated.

########## 4. SoAdSocketTcpAutoConnectTimeout

- **UUID**: `9bc8d70a-8a69-4fe5-aeb3-85c0de0c74f8`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSocketTcpAutoConnectTimeout`
- **数据类型**: FLOAT
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `0`
- **取值范围**: [0..4294967]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This value specifies the time in seconds how long TCP connection attempts are repeated to reach SOAD_SOCON_ONLINE. This parameter is restricted to socket connection groups which are initiating a TCP connection and are under control of SoAd (SoAdSocketAutomaticSoConSetup and SoAdSocketTcpInitiate are enabled in SoAdSocketConnectionGroup).

Note 1: A value of 0 means that the feature is disabled.
Note 2: This parameter and the related functionality is introduced with ASR R21-11.

######## 引用关系

此容器包含 **2** 个引用：

######### REFERENCE 类型引用

########## 1. SoAdSocketTcpTlsConnectionRef

- **UUID**: `ac8ea004-a919-400d-b84a-d4e63a44fd35`
- **引用名**: `SoAdSocketTcpTlsConnectionRef`
- **引用类型**: REFERENCE
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **目标**: `TcpIpTlsConnection`
- **完整路径**: `/MICROSAR/TcpIp/TcpIpConfig/TcpIpTlsConfig/TcpIpTlsConnection`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: Reference to a TLS connection. If set the TCP socket shall use TLS according to the properties of the TLS connection. The SoAd calls TcpIp_ChangeParameter to apply the TLS connection parameter at runtime.

########## 2. SoAdSocketTCPOptionFilterRef

- **UUID**: `f0506066-cc72-4159-81e3-8b9a9377d113`
- **引用名**: `SoAdSocketTCPOptionFilterRef`
- **引用类型**: REFERENCE
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **目标**: `TcpIpTcpConfigOptionFilter`
- **完整路径**: `/AUTOSAR/EcucDefs/TcpIp/TcpIpConfig/TcpIpTcpConfigOptionFilter`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: Reference to TCP option filter which shall be applied on the related socket.

This parameter is not yet supported and its value is not evaluated but the parameter exists for compatibility reasons.

####### 8. SoAdSocketUdp

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `78d1300d-b663-4bbb-ab36-90f1a2fab335`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `SoAdSocketUdp`
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **需要索引**: false
- **多配置容器**: false

**容器描述**:

Specifies that UDP is used as transport protocol for the socket connection group and parameters only related to UDP socket connections.

######## 参数配置

此容器包含 **12** 个参数：

######### INTEGER 类型参数

########## 1. SoAdSocketnPduUdpTxBufferMin

- **UUID**: `d9d68db9-6563-465e-affa-d4d42a2e5beb`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSocketnPduUdpTxBufferMin`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `32`
- **取值范围**: [0..65535]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This value specifies the amount of data in bytes (PDU data provided by the upper layer and PDU Header if used) the SoAd shall be able to buffer for data transmission via this socket connection in case the UDP message shall be buffered for transmission of multiple PDUs per UDP (nPduUdpTxBuffer).

This parameter must not exist if no nPduUdpTxBuffer is used.
Note: in case of a UDP socket and an upper layer with TP API or an upper layer with IF API with UDP transmit retry (for single PDUs) configured, the required buffer size can be determined automatically.

########## 2. SoAdSocketUdpImmediateIfTxConfirmationListSize

- **UUID**: `92a6c3a3-0c04-43d6-836b-2cb473de4958`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSocketUdpImmediateIfTxConfirmationListSize`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `1`
- **取值范围**: [1..255]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This value specifies the size of a list which is used in the TcpIp module to handle UDP transmissions with TxConfirmation. 
Please refer to TcpIp parameter "TcpIpUdpTxReqListSize" for more details.

This parameter is used if "SoAdSocketUdpImmediateIfTxConfirmation" is enabled.

########## 3. SoAdSocketnPduUdpTxQueueSize

- **UUID**: `e23edd63-9e0d-4cff-99be-595f7877da43`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSocketnPduUdpTxQueueSize`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `2`
- **取值范围**: [1..255]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This value specifies the size of a management queue for each socket connection. This queue is used to manage PDUs in the nPduUdpTxBuffer and PDU transmission requests for trigger transmit. Both transmission types are stored within one queue, so make sure that size is sufficient for all related SoAdPduRoutes.

This parameter must not exist if no related SoAdPduRoute exisits with enabled SoAdTxIfTriggerTransmit.

########## 4. SoAdSocketUdpAddressResolutionRetryQueueLimit

- **UUID**: `6221fe99-556f-4b3d-b9b1-c1e406268e82`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSocketUdpAddressResolutionRetryQueueLimit`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `1`
- **取值范围**: [0..255]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This value specifies the limitation of maximum used UDP transmission retry queue elements for this socket. 
The value is forwarded to the TcpIp module on socket creation.
Example: A transmission over a UDP socket fails in TcpIp module since address resolution (ARP) is not finished yet. This transmission is stored in the retry queue and the transmission request is assumed as successful. If the limitation is set to 1 TcpIp will reject further transmission requests until retry queue element is released.

######### BOOLEAN 类型参数

########## 1. SoAdSocketUdpListenOnly

- **UUID**: `71152800-71f8-4175-86d6-11f9c61564ae`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSocketUdpListenOnly`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter defines if the socket connection group is only used for reception (TRUE) or used for both reception and transmission (FALSE).

########## 2. SoAdSocketUdpStrictHeaderLenCheckEnabled

- **UUID**: `72d25a79-49e9-4444-907d-7b6b8bf85614`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSocketUdpStrictHeaderLenCheckEnabled`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter defines if UDP messages shall be dropped (TRUE) if the length of all contained PDUs does not match the length of the whole message or not (FALSE). Shall only be set to TRUE if SoAdPduHeaderEnable is also set to TRUE.

########## 3. SoAdSocketUdpChecksumEnabled

- **UUID**: `77bab4af-6816-424f-ba2a-5778e2e2dca0`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSocketUdpChecksumEnabled`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `true`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter specifies if UDP checksum calculation shall be enabled (TRUE) or disabled (FALSE) on the related socket. FALSE implies that the upper layer of the socket connection is either capable to handle malformed messages or applies a checksum mechanism itself.

Note:
This parameter is ignored when SoAdSocketUdpChecksumChangeEnabled is disabled.

When the parameter is set to FALSE the following applies:
 Rx: Checksum of 0 is accepted and the frame is forwarded. Checksums unequal to 0 will be checked.
 Tx: Checksum is not calculated and the value is set to 0.

########## 4. SoAdSocketUdpAddressResolutionRetryEnabled

- **UUID**: `8d4c918f-c16f-45da-a583-336c1843ed67`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSocketUdpAddressResolutionRetryEnabled`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `true`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter defines if a transmission request shall be stored in TcpIp module to retry transmission in case of missing address resolution (ARP/NDP).
True: Retry enabled
False: Retry disabled

########## 5. SoAdSocketUdpImmediateIfTxConfirmation

- **UUID**: `0d7bf58c-c61b-484c-ab23-2011df725c2c`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSocketUdpImmediateIfTxConfirmation`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter defines the ability to notify the IF upper layers via TX Confirmation in context of Ethernet driver's TX Confirmation.

All upper layers using a Socket Connection of a Socket Connection Group configured for UDP communication with this feature enabled will be notified by the TX Confirmation in context of Ethernet driver's TX Confirmation.

false:    Upper Layers are notified by the TX Confirmation called in next main function cycle of SoAd (Task-Context). The call is independent from the TX Confirmation by the lower layer of SoAd.
true:    Upper Layers are notified by the TX Confirmation propagated through the Ethernet Stack starting by the Ethernet Driver (Task- or Interrupt-Context).

########## 6. SoAdSocketUdpChecksumChangeEnabled

- **UUID**: `3c6ecc0f-c3db-467a-bd1e-6698db2394c1`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSocketUdpChecksumChangeEnabled`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter defines if UDP checksum calculation setting shall be changed (TRUE) or not (FALSE) on the related socket. If enabled, the SoAd calls TcpIp_ChangeParameter with the corresponding value of SoAdSocketUdpChecksumEnabled to apply the UDP checksum calculation configuration at runtime.

Note:
This parameter must be disabled when...
...UDP frames are used via IPv6. A checksum is mandatory in this case.
...vBsdAd TcpIp is used (SoAdVBsdAdUsed).

Additionally, when UDP checksum offloading is done in hardware (EthCtrlEnableOffloadChecksumUDP) this parameter is usually expected to be disabled. If you enable it be aware that HW could overwrite checksums of '0' with a valid calculated checksum which can not be detected by SW.

######### FLOAT 类型参数

########## 1. SoAdSocketUdpAliveSupervisionTimeout

- **UUID**: `ee9cea99-da8e-406d-bc2f-724a1f513b16`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSocketUdpAliveSupervisionTimeout`
- **数据类型**: FLOAT
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `1`
- **取值范围**: [0..65535]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This value specifies the time in [s] a UDP socket connection remains in the mode SOAD_SOCON_ONLINE after the latest reception of a frame from the remote peer specified by the remote address. If this optional parameter is not enabled UDP Alive Supervision is deactivated for the related socket connection group.

Note: In case the timeout is configured to a value smaller than the SoAdMainFunctionPeriod the timeout is set to one main funtion cycle.

########## 2. SoAdSocketUdpTriggerTimeout

- **UUID**: `6033cc52-fffa-433b-a5d5-74879ea83812`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSocketUdpTriggerTimeout`
- **数据类型**: FLOAT
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `2`
- **取值范围**: [0..65535]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This value specifies the timeout in [s] a nPduUdpTxBuffer is waiting for a PDU with TriggerMode = TRIGGER_ALWAYS, i.e. when the timeout expires the nPduUdpTxBuffer is transmitted. Timer is reset after each UDP transmission. This optional parameter is only relevant if a nPduUdpTxBuffer is used.

Note: In case the timeout is configured to a value smaller than the SoAdMainFunctionPeriod the timeout is set to one main funtion cycle.

######### 1. SoAdEventQueueOptimization

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `c2c36996-8ae4-496f-85ac-961f1222bdb4`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `SoAdEventQueueOptimization`
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **需要索引**: false

**容器描述**:

This container provides optimization parameter for module internal event queues.

########## 参数配置

此容器包含 **6** 个参数：

########### INTEGER 类型参数

############ 1. SoAdEventQueueLimitTpRxSoCon

- **UUID**: `be107feb-92fe-4ae7-9a2c-6e7eed0958f7`
- **参数名**: `SoAdEventQueueLimitTpRxSoCon`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `1`
- **取值范围**: [1..65535]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter limits the number of handled TP receptions on socket connections per main function. If more TP receptions are active they will be handled in further main functions. This parameter can be used to minimize main function runtime but may lead to performance losses for TP receptions.

############ 2. SoAdEventQueueLimitStateSoCon

- **UUID**: `e06572cb-7943-48b0-ae94-efda71fca63c`
- **参数名**: `SoAdEventQueueLimitStateSoCon`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `1`
- **取值范围**: [1..65535]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter limits the number of handled socket connection events per main function (e.g. after remote address change). If more socket connection events have to be handled they will be handled in further main functions. This parameter can be used to minimize main function runtime but may lead to performance losses on socket connections state change (e.g. open/close).

############ 3. SoAdEventQueueLimitIfUdpPduRoute

- **UUID**: `9cced69b-352e-4362-a957-7c2aaaa07d23`
- **参数名**: `SoAdEventQueueLimitIfUdpPduRoute`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `1`
- **取值范围**: [1..65535]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter limits the number of handled IF TxConfirmations on PduRoutes per main function. If more TxConfirmations are pending they will be handled in further main functions. This parameter can be used to minimize main function runtime but may lead to performance losses for IF PduRoutes since TxConfirmation may be called delayed.

############ 4. SoAdEventQueueLimitIfTxRouteGrp

- **UUID**: `f6af4bb8-305a-41d9-8801-b60ee24724c8`
- **参数名**: `SoAdEventQueueLimitIfTxRouteGrp`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `1`
- **取值范围**: [1..65535]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter limits the number of handled IF Routing Group transmissions per main function. If more transmissions are pending they will be handled in further main functions. This parameter can be used to minimize main function runtime but may lead to performance losses for IF Routing Group transmissions.

############ 5. SoAdEventQueueLimitTpTxSoCon

- **UUID**: `9f790fef-e3bc-4fcf-8e0d-fb659fe0f2e0`
- **参数名**: `SoAdEventQueueLimitTpTxSoCon`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `1`
- **取值范围**: [1..65535]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter limits the number of handled TP transmissions on socket connections per main function. If more transmissions are active they will be handled in further main functions. This parameter can be used to minimize main function runtime but may lead to performance losses for TP transmissions.

############ 6. SoAdEventQueueLimitSoConModeChg

- **UUID**: `8d581a5c-64a2-4226-959c-2ae1cf0f7e1e`
- **参数名**: `SoAdEventQueueLimitSoConModeChg`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `1`
- **取值范围**: [1..65535]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter limits the number of handled socket connections per main function for which the SoConModeChg callback is pending. If more socket connections have pending SoConModeChg callbacks they will be handled in further main functions. This parameter can be used to minimize main function runtime but may lead to delayed reporting of SoConModeChg.
