- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: Reference to TCP option filter which shall be applied on the related socket.

This parameter is not yet supported and its value is not evaluated but the parameter exists for compatibility reasons.

##### 10. SoAdSocketUdp

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `78d1300d-b663-4bbb-ab36-90f1a2fab335`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `SoAdSocketUdp`
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **需要索引**: false
- **多配置容器**: false

**容器描述**:

Specifies that UDP is used as transport protocol for the socket connection group and parameters only related to UDP socket connections.

###### 参数配置

此容器包含 **12** 个参数：

####### INTEGER 类型参数

######## 1. SoAdSocketnPduUdpTxBufferMin

- **UUID**: `d9d68db9-6563-465e-affa-d4d42a2e5beb`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSocketnPduUdpTxBufferMin`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `32`
- **取值范围**: [0..65535]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This value specifies the amount of data in bytes (PDU data provided by the upper layer and PDU Header if used) the SoAd shall be able to buffer for data transmission via this socket connection in case the UDP message shall be buffered for transmission of multiple PDUs per UDP (nPduUdpTxBuffer).

This parameter must not exist if no nPduUdpTxBuffer is used.
Note: in case of a UDP socket and an upper layer with TP API or an upper layer with IF API with UDP transmit retry (for single PDUs) configured, the required buffer size can be determined automatically.

######## 2. SoAdSocketUdpImmediateIfTxConfirmationListSize

- **UUID**: `92a6c3a3-0c04-43d6-836b-2cb473de4958`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSocketUdpImmediateIfTxConfirmationListSize`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `1`
- **取值范围**: [1..255]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This value specifies the size of a list which is used in the TcpIp module to handle UDP transmissions with TxConfirmation. 
Please refer to TcpIp parameter "TcpIpUdpTxReqListSize" for more details.

This parameter is used if "SoAdSocketUdpImmediateIfTxConfirmation" is enabled.

######## 3. SoAdSocketnPduUdpTxQueueSize

- **UUID**: `e23edd63-9e0d-4cff-99be-595f7877da43`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSocketnPduUdpTxQueueSize`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `2`
- **取值范围**: [1..255]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This value specifies the size of a management queue for each socket connection. This queue is used to manage PDUs in the nPduUdpTxBuffer and PDU transmission requests for trigger transmit. Both transmission types are stored within one queue, so make sure that size is sufficient for all related SoAdPduRoutes.

This parameter must not exist if no related SoAdPduRoute exisits with enabled SoAdTxIfTriggerTransmit.

######## 4. SoAdSocketUdpAddressResolutionRetryQueueLimit

- **UUID**: `6221fe99-556f-4b3d-b9b1-c1e406268e82`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSocketUdpAddressResolutionRetryQueueLimit`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `1`
- **取值范围**: [0..255]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This value specifies the limitation of maximum used UDP transmission retry queue elements for this socket. 
The value is forwarded to the TcpIp module on socket creation.
Example: A transmission over a UDP socket fails in TcpIp module since address resolution (ARP) is not finished yet. This transmission is stored in the retry queue and the transmission request is assumed as successful. If the limitation is set to 1 TcpIp will reject further transmission requests until retry queue element is released.

####### BOOLEAN 类型参数

######## 1. SoAdSocketUdpListenOnly

- **UUID**: `71152800-71f8-4175-86d6-11f9c61564ae`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSocketUdpListenOnly`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter defines if the socket connection group is only used for reception (TRUE) or used for both reception and transmission (FALSE).

######## 2. SoAdSocketUdpStrictHeaderLenCheckEnabled

- **UUID**: `72d25a79-49e9-4444-907d-7b6b8bf85614`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSocketUdpStrictHeaderLenCheckEnabled`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter defines if UDP messages shall be dropped (TRUE) if the length of all contained PDUs does not match the length of the whole message or not (FALSE). Shall only be set to TRUE if SoAdPduHeaderEnable is also set to TRUE.

######## 3. SoAdSocketUdpChecksumEnabled

- **UUID**: `77bab4af-6816-424f-ba2a-5778e2e2dca0`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSocketUdpChecksumEnabled`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `true`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter specifies if UDP checksum calculation shall be enabled (TRUE) or disabled (FALSE) on the related socket. FALSE implies that the upper layer of the socket connection is either capable to handle malformed messages or applies a checksum mechanism itself.

Note:
This parameter is ignored when SoAdSocketUdpChecksumChangeEnabled is disabled.

When the parameter is set to FALSE the following applies:
 Rx: Checksum of 0 is accepted and the frame is forwarded. Checksums unequal to 0 will be checked.
 Tx: Checksum is not calculated and the value is set to 0.

######## 4. SoAdSocketUdpAddressResolutionRetryEnabled

- **UUID**: `8d4c918f-c16f-45da-a583-336c1843ed67`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSocketUdpAddressResolutionRetryEnabled`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `true`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter defines if a transmission request shall be stored in TcpIp module to retry transmission in case of missing address resolution (ARP/NDP).
True: Retry enabled
False: Retry disabled

######## 5. SoAdSocketUdpImmediateIfTxConfirmation

- **UUID**: `0d7bf58c-c61b-484c-ab23-2011df725c2c`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSocketUdpImmediateIfTxConfirmation`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter defines the ability to notify the IF upper layers via TX Confirmation in context of Ethernet driver's TX Confirmation.

All upper layers using a Socket Connection of a Socket Connection Group configured for UDP communication with this feature enabled will be notified by the TX Confirmation in context of Ethernet driver's TX Confirmation.

false:    Upper Layers are notified by the TX Confirmation called in next main function cycle of SoAd (Task-Context). The call is independent from the TX Confirmation by the lower layer of SoAd.
true:    Upper Layers are notified by the TX Confirmation propagated through the Ethernet Stack starting by the Ethernet Driver (Task- or Interrupt-Context).

######## 6. SoAdSocketUdpChecksumChangeEnabled

- **UUID**: `3c6ecc0f-c3db-467a-bd1e-6698db2394c1`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSocketUdpChecksumChangeEnabled`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter defines if UDP checksum calculation setting shall be changed (TRUE) or not (FALSE) on the related socket. If enabled, the SoAd calls TcpIp_ChangeParameter with the corresponding value of SoAdSocketUdpChecksumEnabled to apply the UDP checksum calculation configuration at runtime.

Note:
This parameter must be disabled when...
...UDP frames are used via IPv6. A checksum is mandatory in this case.
...vBsdAd TcpIp is used (SoAdVBsdAdUsed).

Additionally, when UDP checksum offloading is done in hardware (EthCtrlEnableOffloadChecksumUDP) this parameter is usually expected to be disabled. If you enable it be aware that HW could overwrite checksums of '0' with a valid calculated checksum which can not be detected by SW.

####### FLOAT 类型参数

######## 1. SoAdSocketUdpAliveSupervisionTimeout

- **UUID**: `ee9cea99-da8e-406d-bc2f-724a1f513b16`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSocketUdpAliveSupervisionTimeout`
- **数据类型**: FLOAT
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `1`
- **取值范围**: [0..65535]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This value specifies the time in [s] a UDP socket connection remains in the mode SOAD_SOCON_ONLINE after the latest reception of a frame from the remote peer specified by the remote address. If this optional parameter is not enabled UDP Alive Supervision is deactivated for the related socket connection group.

Note: In case the timeout is configured to a value smaller than the SoAdMainFunctionPeriod the timeout is set to one main funtion cycle.

######## 2. SoAdSocketUdpTriggerTimeout

- **UUID**: `6033cc52-fffa-433b-a5d5-74879ea83812`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSocketUdpTriggerTimeout`
- **数据类型**: FLOAT
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `2`
- **取值范围**: [0..65535]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This value specifies the timeout in [s] a nPduUdpTxBuffer is waiting for a PDU with TriggerMode = TRIGGER_ALWAYS, i.e. when the timeout expires the nPduUdpTxBuffer is transmitted. Timer is reset after each UDP transmission. This optional parameter is only relevant if a nPduUdpTxBuffer is used.

Note: In case the timeout is configured to a value smaller than the SoAdMainFunctionPeriod the timeout is set to one main funtion cycle.

##### 11. SoAdSocketRoute

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `d3ea7ccb-83e6-486f-b6b4-b2ff9635ac39`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `SoAdSocketRoute`
- **多重性**: [0..infinite]
- **需要索引**: false
- **多配置容器**: false

**容器描述**:

Describes the path of a PDU from a socket in the TCP/IP stack to an upper layer of the SoAd after reception in the TCP/IP Stack.

###### 参数配置

此容器包含 **1** 个参数：

####### INTEGER 类型参数

######## 1. SoAdRxPduHeaderId

- **UUID**: `bddb9d21-5c2b-4198-8b09-f2b4d58f01dc`
- **参数名**: `SoAdRxPduHeaderId`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `0`
- **取值范围**: [0..4294967296]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This value specifies the ID contained in the packet received on the TCP/IP connection if the PDU header option is enabled.

###### 引用关系

此容器包含 **1** 个引用：

####### CHOICE-REFERENCE 类型引用

######## 1. SoAdRxSocketConnOrSocketConnBundleRef

- **UUID**: `5dbb92a5-7cb8-40e5-93be-e6b1251d4a78`
- **引用名**: `SoAdRxSocketConnOrSocketConnBundleRef`
- **引用类型**: CHOICE-REFERENCE
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **需要索引**: false
- **目标引用列表**:
  - `/AUTOSAR/EcucDefs/SoAd/SoAdConfig/SoAdSocketConnectionGroup/SoAdSocketConnection`
  - `/AUTOSAR/EcucDefs/SoAd/SoAdConfig/SoAdSocketConnectionGroup`
- **描述**: Choice Reference to a SocketConnection or to a SocketConnectionGroup on which the PDU was received. The reference to a SocketConnectionGroup shall only be used for upper layers with IF API.

###### 配置容器

本模块包含 **1** 个配置容器：

####### 1. SoAdSocketRouteDest

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `7a21c944-a675-43c1-8615-b604f6096676`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `SoAdSocketRouteDest`
- **多重性**: [1..infinite]
- **需要索引**: false
- **多配置容器**: false

**容器描述**:

Describes the upper layer destination PDU for a message received on a TcpIp socket.

######## 参数配置

此容器包含 **2** 个参数：

######### INTEGER 类型参数

########## 1. SoAdRxPduId

- **UUID**: `12859264-e953-4156-a2f8-1db021dc7198`
- **参数名**: `SoAdRxPduId`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `0`
- **取值范围**: [0..65535]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: true
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This value specifies a unique identifier which is used for a receive cancellation request from an upper layer of the SoAd.

######### ENUMERATION 类型参数

########## 1. SoAdRxUpperLayerType

- **UUID**: `c096319f-c53c-4eaf-884c-a94f7f683d6f`
- **参数名**: `SoAdRxUpperLayerType`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `IF`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `IF` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
  - `TP` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
- **描述**: This parameter defines the upper layer interface type (must be "IF" in case of multiple RxPdus).

######## 引用关系

此容器包含 **2** 个引用：

######### REFERENCE 类型引用

########## 1. SoAdRxPduRef

- **UUID**: `0fe04701-7ea5-4e09-a838-6d93624c1f0f`
- **引用名**: `SoAdRxPduRef`
- **引用类型**: REFERENCE
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **目标**: `Pdu`
- **完整路径**: `/AUTOSAR/EcucDefs/EcuC/EcucPduCollection/Pdu`

##### 13. SoAdInstance

###### 配置容器

本模块包含 **8** 个配置容器：

####### 1. SoAdEventQueueOptimization

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `c2c36996-8ae4-496f-85ac-961f1222bdb4`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `SoAdEventQueueOptimization`
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **需要索引**: false

**容器描述**:

This container provides optimization parameter for module internal event queues.

######## 参数配置

此容器包含 **6** 个参数：

######### INTEGER 类型参数

########## 1. SoAdEventQueueLimitTpRxSoCon

- **UUID**: `be107feb-92fe-4ae7-9a2c-6e7eed0958f7`
- **参数名**: `SoAdEventQueueLimitTpRxSoCon`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `1`
- **取值范围**: [1..65535]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter limits the number of handled TP receptions on socket connections per main function. If more TP receptions are active they will be handled in further main functions. This parameter can be used to minimize main function runtime but may lead to performance losses for TP receptions.

########## 2. SoAdEventQueueLimitStateSoCon

- **UUID**: `e06572cb-7943-48b0-ae94-efda71fca63c`
- **参数名**: `SoAdEventQueueLimitStateSoCon`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `1`
- **取值范围**: [1..65535]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter limits the number of handled socket connection events per main function (e.g. after remote address change). If more socket connection events have to be handled they will be handled in further main functions. This parameter can be used to minimize main function runtime but may lead to performance losses on socket connections state change (e.g. open/close).

########## 3. SoAdEventQueueLimitIfUdpPduRoute

- **UUID**: `9cced69b-352e-4362-a957-7c2aaaa07d23`
- **参数名**: `SoAdEventQueueLimitIfUdpPduRoute`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `1`
- **取值范围**: [1..65535]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter limits the number of handled IF TxConfirmations on PduRoutes per main function. If more TxConfirmations are pending they will be handled in further main functions. This parameter can be used to minimize main function runtime but may lead to performance losses for IF PduRoutes since TxConfirmation may be called delayed.

########## 4. SoAdEventQueueLimitIfTxRouteGrp

- **UUID**: `f6af4bb8-305a-41d9-8801-b60ee24724c8`
- **参数名**: `SoAdEventQueueLimitIfTxRouteGrp`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `1`
- **取值范围**: [1..65535]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter limits the number of handled IF Routing Group transmissions per main function. If more transmissions are pending they will be handled in further main functions. This parameter can be used to minimize main function runtime but may lead to performance losses for IF Routing Group transmissions.

########## 5. SoAdEventQueueLimitTpTxSoCon

- **UUID**: `9f790fef-e3bc-4fcf-8e0d-fb659fe0f2e0`
- **参数名**: `SoAdEventQueueLimitTpTxSoCon`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `1`
- **取值范围**: [1..65535]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter limits the number of handled TP transmissions on socket connections per main function. If more transmissions are active they will be handled in further main functions. This parameter can be used to minimize main function runtime but may lead to performance losses for TP transmissions.

########## 6. SoAdEventQueueLimitSoConModeChg

- **UUID**: `8d581a5c-64a2-4226-959c-2ae1cf0f7e1e`
- **参数名**: `SoAdEventQueueLimitSoConModeChg`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `1`
- **取值范围**: [1..65535]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter limits the number of handled socket connections per main function for which the SoConModeChg callback is pending. If more socket connections have pending SoConModeChg callbacks they will be handled in further main functions. This parameter can be used to minimize main function runtime but may lead to delayed reporting of SoConModeChg.

####### 2. SoAdTimeoutListOptimization

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `02fa92c5-a429-4107-a2bf-914aa38bb862`

**容器描述**:

This container provides optimization parameter for module internal timeout lists.

######## 参数配置

此容器包含 **1** 个参数：

######### INTEGER 类型参数

########## 1. SoAdTimeoutListLimitNPduUdpTx

- **UUID**: `2f97e135-eb68-4453-a54f-343b134875ad`
- **参数名**: `SoAdTimeoutListLimitNPduUdpTx`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `1`
- **取值范围**: [1..65535]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter limits the number of parallel nPdu transmissions per main function. All further transmission requests are rejected if list is filled. This parameter can be used to minimize main function runtime and RAM.

####### 3. SoAdIpConfig

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `299591db-1bc1-49d9-96d4-c60d52ce545a`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `SoAdIpConfig`
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **需要索引**: false

**容器描述**:

This container provides IP related configuration parameter.

######## 配置容器

本模块包含 **1** 个配置容器：

######### 1. SoAdIpFragmentationBuffer

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `d2d83038-1669-446d-bbfa-42119c378739`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `SoAdIpFragmentationBuffer`
- **多重性**: [0..65535]
- **需要索引**: false

**容器描述**:

This container provides parameter to configure an IP fragmentation buffer.

An IP fragmentation buffer is used when a SoAdPduRoute configures SoAdTxIfTriggerTransmit to true and no nPduUdpTxBuffer is used on a UDP socket. In this case a buffer is required since TcpIp copies the PDU fragmented (multiple SoAd API calls) but TriggerTransmit API allows only one call.

The IP fragmentation buffers are shared by all socket connections of an instance. A transmission request fails when no buffer is available at the moment.

########## 参数配置

此容器包含 **1** 个参数：

########### INTEGER 类型参数

############ 1. SoAdIpFragmentationBufferSize

- **UUID**: `bc7d9b64-7de4-4940-bf82-6bbbfc1ec49f`
- **参数名**: `SoAdIpFragmentationBufferSize`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `3000`
- **取值范围**: [1..65535]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter defines the size of the IP fragmentation buffer.

####### 4. SoAdIpFragmentationBuffer

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `d2d83038-1669-446d-bbfa-42119c378739`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER

- **多重性**: [0..65535]

The IP fragmentation buffers are shared by all socket connections of an instance. A transmission request fails when no buffer is available at the moment.

- **容器名称**: `SoAdIfTriggerTransmitBuffer`
- **多重性**: [1..65535]
- **需要索引**: false

**容器描述**:

This container provides parameter to configure a trigger transmit buffer.
A trigger transmit buffer is used whenever PDU data has to be stored in SoAd before transmission is possible and SoAdTxIfTriggerTransmit is enabled. This applies for example if SoAdTxIfTriggerTransmitForceSingleCall is enabled for a PDU fan-out or for transmissions via TCP.

- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `SoAdIfMetaDataBuffer`
- **多重性**: [1..65535]
- **需要索引**: false

**容器描述**:

This container provides parameter to configure a meta data buffer.
A meta data buffer is used whenever it is requested to forward the socket connection on reception. The mechanism appends the socket connection index as meta data to a received PDU. When meta data is used for TCP and UDP on an instance, it is recommended to configure at least 2 meta data buffers.

- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter defines the size of the IF meta data buffer.

- **描述**: This parameter limits the number of handled TP transmissions on socket connections per main function. If more transmissions are active they will be handled in further main functions. This parameter can be used to minimize main function runtime but may lead to performance losses for TP transmissions.

######## 6. SoAdEventQueueLimitSoConModeChg

- **UUID**: `8d581a5c-64a2-4226-959c-2ae1cf0f7e1e`
- **参数名**: `SoAdEventQueueLimitSoConModeChg`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `1`
- **取值范围**: [1..65535]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter limits the number of handled socket connections per main function for which the SoConModeChg callback is pending. If more socket connections have pending SoConModeChg callbacks they will be handled in further main functions. This parameter can be used to minimize main function runtime but may lead to delayed reporting of SoConModeChg.

##### 15. SoAdTimeoutListOptimization

This container provides optimization parameter for module internal timeout lists.

####### INTEGER 类型参数

######## 1. SoAdTimeoutListLimitNPduUdpTx

- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `1`
- **取值范围**: [1..65535]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter limits the number of parallel nPdu transmissions per main function. All further transmission requests are rejected if list is filled. This parameter can be used to minimize main function runtime and RAM.

##### 16. SoAdIpConfig

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `299591db-1bc1-49d9-96d4-c60d52ce545a`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `SoAdIpConfig`
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **需要索引**: false

**容器描述**:

This container provides IP related configuration parameter.

###### 配置容器

本模块包含 **1** 个配置容器：

####### 1. SoAdIpFragmentationBuffer

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `d2d83038-1669-446d-bbfa-42119c378739`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `SoAdIpFragmentationBuffer`
- **多重性**: [0..65535]
- **需要索引**: false

**容器描述**:

This container provides parameter to configure an IP fragmentation buffer.

An IP fragmentation buffer is used when a SoAdPduRoute configures SoAdTxIfTriggerTransmit to true and no nPduUdpTxBuffer is used on a UDP socket. In this case a buffer is required since TcpIp copies the PDU fragmented (multiple SoAd API calls) but TriggerTransmit API allows only one call.

The IP fragmentation buffers are shared by all socket connections of an instance. A transmission request fails when no buffer is available at the moment.

######## 参数配置

此容器包含 **1** 个参数：

######### INTEGER 类型参数

########## 1. SoAdIpFragmentationBufferSize

- **UUID**: `bc7d9b64-7de4-4940-bf82-6bbbfc1ec49f`
- **参数名**: `SoAdIpFragmentationBufferSize`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `3000`
- **取值范围**: [1..65535]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter defines the size of the IP fragmentation buffer.

- **多重性**: [0..65535]
- **需要索引**: false

An IP fragmentation buffer is used when a SoAdPduRoute configures SoAdTxIfTriggerTransmit to true and no nPduUdpTxBuffer is used on a UDP socket. In this case a buffer is required since TcpIp copies the PDU fragmented (multiple SoAd API calls) but TriggerTransmit API allows only one call.

The IP fragmentation buffers are shared by all socket connections of an instance. A transmission request fails when no buffer is available at the moment.

##### 18. SoAdIfTriggerTransmitConfig

**容器属性**:

- **容器名称**: `SoAdIfTriggerTransmitConfig`
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **需要索引**: false

This container provides trigger transmit related configuration parameter.

本模块包含 **1** 个配置容器：

####### 1. SoAdIfTriggerTransmitBuffer

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `6c2883f2-8638-4edc-aca2-2a8d60494199`

- **容器名称**: `SoAdIfTriggerTransmitBuffer`
- **多重性**: [1..65535]
- **需要索引**: false

**容器描述**:

This container provides parameter to configure a trigger transmit buffer.
A trigger transmit buffer is used whenever PDU data has to be stored in SoAd before transmission is possible and SoAdTxIfTriggerTransmit is enabled. This applies for example if SoAdTxIfTriggerTransmitForceSingleCall is enabled for a PDU fan-out or for transmissions via TCP.

- **描述**: This parameter defines the size of the trigger transmit buffer.

##### 20. SoAdMetaDataConfig

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `719b8ee0-2dfd-4ede-ac95-41d97b4a1eb2`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `SoAdMetaDataConfig`
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **需要索引**: false

**容器描述**:

This container provides meta data related configuration parameter.

###### 配置容器

本模块包含 **1** 个配置容器：

####### 1. SoAdIfMetaDataBuffer

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `825c68d1-5167-4132-9e9c-a27744cc48bf`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `SoAdIfMetaDataBuffer`
- **多重性**: [1..65535]
- **需要索引**: false

**容器描述**:

This container provides parameter to configure a meta data buffer.
A meta data buffer is used whenever it is requested to forward the socket connection on reception. The mechanism appends the socket connection index as meta data to a received PDU. When meta data is used for TCP and UDP on an instance, it is recommended to configure at least 2 meta data buffers.

######## 参数配置

此容器包含 **1** 个参数：

- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `1500`
- **取值范围**: [1..65535]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter defines the size of the IF meta data buffer.

##### 22. SoAdGeneral

**容器描述**:

This container contains all global configuration parameters of SoAd.

###### 参数配置

此容器包含 **21** 个参数：

####### INTEGER 类型参数

######## 1. SoAdRoutingGroupMax

- **UUID**: `35981583-e0e8-49e0-853d-25100fef97e7`
- **参数名**: `SoAdRoutingGroupMax`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `0`
- **取值范围**: [0..65535]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: Specifies the maximum number of SoAd routing groups.

######## 2. SoAdSoConMax

- **UUID**: `fd87cc44-9a0e-455c-be48-7e1b4f7bc236`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSoConMax`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `0`
- **取值范围**: [0..65535]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: Count of configured SoAd Socket Connections.

######## 3. SoAdRxTcpBufferSegmentsMax

- **UUID**: `d487a20b-ef44-4b29-b831-05a36782652f`
- **参数名**: `SoAdRxTcpBufferSegmentsMax`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `2`
- **取值范围**: [2..255]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter defines the maximum amount of supported TCP buffer segments on reception.

######## 4. SoAdSoConModeChgQueueSize

- **UUID**: `022713ae-f271-41bf-b903-101c84a9f263`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSoConModeChgQueueSize`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `2`
- **取值范围**: [0..255]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This value specifies the amount of mode changes that is stored per socket connection to be reported in the SoConModeChg callback(s).
In case the value is configured to 0, no queueing mechanism is used.
The required queue size depends on the amount of contexts that may interrupt each other. Usually, a queue size of 2 should be sufficient.

####### BOOLEAN 类型参数

######## 1. SoAdDevErrorDetect

- **UUID**: `cf286f06-9325-4638-bd9c-11fe4bd15aca`
- **参数名**: `SoAdDevErrorDetect`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `true`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: Pre-processor switch for enabling development error detection support.

######## 2. SoAdIPv6AddressEnabled

- **UUID**: `3376d88e-8b48-4809-8180-873a0b7c4096`
- **参数名**: `SoAdIPv6AddressEnabled`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: Allows for increased memory allocation to store IPv6 addresses.
TRUE: Enables support for IPv6 addresses
FALSE: Only IPv4 addresses are supported

######## 3. SoAdVersionInfoApi

- **UUID**: `de157d55-4f9b-4832-8108-70da859c5cfd`
- **参数名**: `SoAdVersionInfoApi`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter defines if the SoAd_GetVersionInfo() API is activated.
TRUE: Enables the SoAd_GetVersionInfo() API.
FALSE: SoAd_GetVersionInfo() API is not included.

######## 4. SoAdTxDynamicLengthEnabled

- **UUID**: `f4559e8f-40c0-4473-abeb-671a60b9fae6`
- **参数名**: `SoAdTxDynamicLengthEnabled`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter defines if data length can be modified or set during transmission process.
In case of transmission via IfRoutingGroupTransmit API no PDU length is known until call to TriggerTransmit callback to retrieve data from upper layer. Therefore, an internal buffer is used to store the PDU first before transmit function of TcpIp module can be called. If this feature is enabled the mentioned buffer is not needed anymore since TcpIp module can handle setting length on transmission.
In case of transmission via IfTriggerTransmit API length can differ between transmission request and TriggerTransmit callback. If this feature is not enabled such transmissions are aborted.
In case of nPdu and if IfTriggerTransmit is enabled on the corresponding SoAdPduRoutes nPduUdpTxBuffer can be optimized and is replaced by a request queue if this feature is enabled.

######## 5. SoAdMainFunctionSplitEnabled

- **UUID**: `e1e27e02-ff9a-42c0-ace0-438eca534828`
- **参数名**: `SoAdMainFunctionSplitEnabled`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter specifies whether SoAd shall split up the SoAd_MainFunction into three functions:

SoAd_MainFunctionRx
SoAd_MainFunctionState
SoAd_MainFunctionTx

This allows optimizing the data reception and data transmission path for a specific use case.

Note: All three MainFunctions must be called from the same task or at least from tasks that do not interrupt each other.

######## 6. SoAdSafeBswChecks

- **UUID**: `8282c8c0-6f7b-4853-86ff-e1f34684d27f`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSafeBswChecks`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: Selects if safety checks are used. These checks improve robustness as e.g. invalid parameters are checked before function is executed. This attribute has to be enabled for safety projects where this component is mapped to an ASIL partition.

######## 7. SoAdGetAndResetMeasurementDataApi

- **UUID**: `9d41dac0-a666-48c2-86e2-8a21d7894961`
- **参数名**: `SoAdGetAndResetMeasurementDataApi`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter enables/disables the function SoAd_GetAndResetMeasurementData() to get/reset the measurement data.

######## 8. SoAdGetAndResetMeasurementDataSomeIpSd

- **UUID**: `65ad1dfd-9afa-4c62-8f86-2ee13d8c7282`
- **参数名**: `SoAdGetAndResetMeasurementDataSomeIpSd`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter enables/disables the SOME/IP and SD measurement data counting invalid service IDs and method IDs inside the PDU header. The measurement is only active if SoAdMeasurementDataSomeIpSdType is configured to SOME_IP or SOME_IP_SD on the SoAdSocketConnectionGroup for which the measurement data shall be active.

This parameter can only be set to TRUE when SoAdGetAndResetMeasurementDataApi is enabled.

######## 9. SoAdEnableSecurityEventReporting

- **UUID**: `f077a1ea-6a32-41f9-8c20-5e86609c60a7`
- **参数名**: `SoAdEnableSecurityEventReporting`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter enables/disables the reporting of security events to the IdsM.

######## 10. SoAdRuntimeErrorReport

- **UUID**: `9a0575e5-e681-4eda-8aef-cf4b65f87560`
- **参数名**: `SoAdRuntimeErrorReport`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `true`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter defines the usage of runtime error reporting.
The parameter shall be true if runtime errors shall be reported.

######## 11. SoAdBmcUsageEnabled

- **UUID**: `7cb2a677-6c50-4c3f-9d99-b611a46c7c39`
- **参数名**: `SoAdBmcUsageEnabled`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `true`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter defines if the Basic Software Multicore Library (Bmc) is used for atomical operations. If the usage is disabled, SoAd uses internal measures to ensure data consistency.

Note: This parameter is only relevant in multi-partition usecase.

######## 12. SoAdResetForForceReleaseEnabled

- **UUID**: `6cd2ed23-4bfb-411c-8602-1f7b2130a6d4`
- **参数名**: `SoAdResetForForceReleaseEnabled`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `true`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter defines the behavior of SoAd_ForceReleaseRemoteAddress. 

TRUE: ForceReleaseRemoteAddr leads to sending a TCP RST.
FALSE: ForceReleaseRemoteAddr leads to sending a TCP FIN.

####### FLOAT 类型参数

######## 1. SoAdMainFunctionPeriod

- **UUID**: `d1470831-7738-47f6-8ecc-00640357993a`
- **参数名**: `SoAdMainFunctionPeriod`
- **数据类型**: FLOAT
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `0.005`
- **取值范围**: [0..65535]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: Determines the frequency at which the SoAd_MainFunction() is called in [s].

######## 2. SoAdShutdownFinishedWaitTime

- **UUID**: `70734081-5fa3-4e52-8300-f9af367f3f45`
- **参数名**: `SoAdShutdownFinishedWaitTime`
- **数据类型**: FLOAT
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `2`
- **取值范围**: [0..4294967295]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter defines a timeout for the shutdown mechanism which is initiated by call to SoAd_Shutdown(). If this timeout expires SoAd will switch to state shutdown even if sockets are not yet closed completely.

####### STRING 类型参数

######## 1. SoAdUserConfigFile

- **UUID**: `0f7dc9b1-cfec-4d58-bd2c-365e3708d701`
- **参数名**: `SoAdUserConfigFile`
- **数据类型**: STRING
- **多重性**: [0..1]

- **需要索引**: false
- **字符串变体**:
  - 默认值: `SoAd_UserCfg.h`
- **描述**: If a file path will be specified, the file is read and its contents are put at the end of the SoAd config file.

######## 2. SoAdHeaderFileInclusion

- **UUID**: `8dfc7d9e-57fc-498b-af77-9796de71fcb7`
- **参数名**: `SoAdHeaderFileInclusion`

  - 最小长度: 0
- **描述**: This parameter defines a list of include header files containing callback declarations (e.g. for TLS callback configuration).
These header files are included in the SoAd_Lcfg.c file.

######## 3. SoAdHeaderFileInclusionExtended

- **UUID**: `4296164b-eaee-470e-ae5f-2c848993691c`
- **参数名**: `SoAdHeaderFileInclusionExtended`
- **数据类型**: STRING
- **多重性**: [0..255]
- **作用域**: LOCAL

####### 4. SoAdVerifyRxPduCallback

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `0c568c61-d9b7-49a6-9cd1-085e5aef3fb3`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `SoAdVerifyRxPduCallback`
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **需要索引**: false

**容器描述**:

This container contains a callback to verify if a Rx PDU is allowed to be received.

The callback forwards local socket address, remote socket address, PDU Header ID and a configured number of PDU data to an user. If callback fails SoAd drops the received PDU completely. Otherwise SoAd forwards the received PDU.

Via SoAdVerifyRxPduEnable the feature can be enabled on each socket connection separately.

Hint: This feature is only available for TP-PDUs with PDU header option on TCP socket connections.

######## 参数配置

此容器包含 **2** 个参数：

######### INTEGER 类型参数

########## 1. SoAdVerifyRxPduMaxDataLength

- **UUID**: `c242a216-201c-4ff9-afba-c6fe850ac1ef`
- **参数名**: `SoAdVerifyRxPduMaxDataLength`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `0`
- **取值范围**: [0..255]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter defines the number of received PDU data which are forwarded to the user for Rx PDU verification.

######### FUNCTION-NAME 类型参数

########## 1. SoAdVerifyRxPdu

- **UUID**: `34ae0de4-d8e4-4af3-bb09-5e931f747cbd`
- **参数名**: `SoAdVerifyRxPdu`

- **多重性**: [1..1]

- **来源**: Vector Informatik

- **需要索引**: false
- **函数变体**:
- **描述**: This parameter defines the callback name for the Rx PDU verification.

########## 1. SoAdReduceBoolDataByNegationThreshold

- **UUID**: `cc4b1ce4-299b-4084-ae56-a5635c178643`
- **参数名**: `SoAdReduceBoolDataByNegationThreshold`

- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter activates/deactivates the capability to compress boolean CONFIG-CLASS PRE-COMPILE ROM data by using the negation operator.

0: The optimization is deactivated.
>0: If the number of deduplicateable elements is greater than this threshold the data optimization can be performed.

########## 2. SoAdReduceBoolDataByNumericalComparisonThreshold

- **UUID**: `5b7a70fa-f91c-4528-a348-ab9475f1dbc2`
- **参数名**: `SoAdReduceBoolDataByNumericalComparisonThreshold`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]

- **取值范围**: [0..9223372036854775807]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter activates/deactivates the capability to compress boolean CONFIG-CLASS PRE-COMPILE ROM data by using numerical comparison with other ROM data.

0: The optimization is deactivated.
>0: If the number of deduplicateable elements is greater than this threshold the data optimization can be performed.

########## 3. SoAdReduceBoolDataByNumericalRelationThreshold

- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter activates/deactivates the capability to compress boolean CONFIG-CLASS PRE-COMPILE ROM data by using relational comparison with other ROM data.

0: The optimization is deactivated.
>0: If the number of deduplicateable elements is greater than this threshold the data optimization can be performed.

########## 4. SoAdReduceNumericalDataByOffsetThreshold

- **UUID**: `b116a9c4-d331-4c00-bd88-fca058014897`
- **参数名**: `SoAdReduceNumericalDataByOffsetThreshold`

- **多重性**: [1..1]

>0: If the number of deduplicateable elements is greater than this threshold the data optimization can be performed.

########## 5. SoAdReduceNumericalDataByArraySubtractionThreshold

- **UUID**: `7f9ff504-5afa-481f-aaf2-405d5133b960`
- **参数名**: `SoAdReduceNumericalDataByArraySubtractionThreshold`

0: The optimization is deactivated.
>0: If the number of deduplicateable elements is greater than this threshold the data optimization can be performed.

######### BOOLEAN 类型参数

########## 1. SoAdOutOfBoundsWriteSanitizer

- **UUID**: `7860e17e-d471-4192-978e-5a273d1ad75b`
- **参数名**: `SoAdOutOfBoundsWriteSanitizer`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter activates/deactivates the generation of runtime checks which call a DET error notification function to find easily out of bounds write problems.

FALSE:  no checks are generated in the data access.
TRUE: the data access is enriched with DET checks to validate indexes.

########## 2. SoAdOutOfBoundsReadSanitizer

- **UUID**: `736f51ca-ccf5-420d-8398-f9d2590bae8a`
- **参数名**: `SoAdOutOfBoundsReadSanitizer`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter activates/deactivates the generation of runtime checks which call a DET error notification function to find easily out of bounds read problems.

FALSE:  no checks are generated in the data access.
TRUE: the data access is enriched with DET checks to validate indexes.

########## 3. SoAdReduceConstantData2Define

- **UUID**: `6179eb43-3818-4bab-82c2-e3cedd1ed1ce`
- **参数名**: `SoAdReduceConstantData2Define`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter activates/deactivates the capability to generate CONFIG-CLASS PRE-COMPILE ROM arrays as constant define.

FALSE: ROM arrays are generated as data even if all values are identical.
TRUE: ROM arrays are generated as constant define if all values are identical.

########## 4. SoAdDeduplicateIndirectedData

- **UUID**: `f82c2094-7716-4295-bd80-8e72364fb4df`
- **参数名**: `SoAdDeduplicateIndirectedData`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter activates/deactivates the capability to compress 0:N relational ROM data in all CONFIG-CLASSES without increasing the runtime.

FALSE: 0:N relational ROM data is not compressed.
TRUE: 0:N relational ROM data is compressed without increasing the runtime.

########## 5. SoAdShortSymbols

- **UUID**: `f2bb9304-4cfd-4588-9b99-fe5cfdce1399`
- **参数名**: `SoAdShortSymbols`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter activates/deactivates the capability to generate shortened symbol names.

FALSE: symbol names are generated in a human readable style based on the MIP, tags and variant names.
TRUE: symbol names are generated based on the MIP and a CRC32.

########## 6. SoAdInterfacesForDeactivatedData

- **UUID**: `e209f3f4-afc2-46cc-92de-d4ee97e5399d`
- **参数名**: `SoAdInterfacesForDeactivatedData`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter activates/deactivates the capability to generate bsw data interfaces for deactivated data elements. This is an advantage for the BSW developer to reduce the time to market with a development environment using auto completition and to investigate potential interfaces.

FALSE: data interfaces are not generated if the data elementis deactivated.
TRUE: data interfaces are generated as e.g. empty macros.

########## 7. SoAdReferringKeysInComments

- **UUID**: `c32a1835-1f3c-4cbf-a510-1a54c4912438`
- **参数名**: `SoAdReferringKeysInComments`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter activates/deactivates the capability to generate referring keys in comments. This is an advantage for the developer to investigate indirections, but this feature reduces the overall readability of the generated data.

FALSE: referring keys are not generated in comments.
TRUE: referring keys are generated in comments.

######### ENUMERATION 类型参数

########## 1. SoAdOutOfBoundsWriteProtectionStrategy

- **UUID**: `d40a0d57-2209-48af-9e31-b5d79d4ef04b`
- **参数名**: `SoAdOutOfBoundsWriteProtectionStrategy`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `NONE`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `NONE` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `INDEX_SATURATION` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `INDEX_CHECKING` (来源: Vector Informatik)
- **描述**: This parameter is used to configure a strategy to protect the code to write out of bounds.

NONE: no protection strategy is generated in the data access.
INDEX_SATURATION: arrays are blown up and the data access index is saturated by an appropriate mask. The advantage is the speed of the data access, but own data elements at other indexes of the same variable can be overridden.
INDEX_CHECKING: the data access index is validated by a runtime check. The advantage is that values are never written to incorrect indexes of the data access.

########## 2. SoAdBoolDataInArrayOfStructStrategy

- **UUID**: `01df6817-6e77-41eb-bdd1-725c395dbe8d`
- **参数名**: `SoAdBoolDataInArrayOfStructStrategy`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `BOOLEAN`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `BOOLEAN` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `BITFIELD` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `BITMASKING` (来源: Vector Informatik)
- **描述**: This parameter is used to tailor the usage of boolean data in structures in all CONFIG-CLASSES ROM data. The difference between BITFIELD and BITMASKING depends on your compiler options and memory mapping.

BOOLEAN: the datatype of boolean data is native boolean.
BITFIELD: the bitfield type is used and the compiler extracts the boolean data from structures.
BITMASKING: generated masks are used to extract the boolean data from structures.

########## 3. SoAdDataDeduplicationStrategy

- **UUID**: `8b6a9646-0482-4399-b545-44e13984665d`
- **参数名**: `SoAdDataDeduplicationStrategy`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `NONE`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `NONE` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `DEDUPLICATE_CONST_DATA_WITHOUT_CAST` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `DEDUPLICATE_CONST_DATA_WITH_CAST` (来源: Vector Informatik)
- **描述**: This parameter is used to taylor the the deduplication of CONFIG-CLASS PRE-COMPILE  ROM data.

NONE: The generated data is not deduplicated.
DEDUPLICATE_CONST_DATA_WITHOUT_CAST: The data is deduplicated without using casts.
DEDUPLICATE_CONST_DATA_WITH_CAST: The data is deduplicated using casts.

########## 4. SoAdReduceBoolDataByNumericalOperandStrategy

- **UUID**: `1892f15e-cddf-4bb4-b8f5-9775cb49e99a`
- **参数名**: `SoAdReduceBoolDataByNumericalOperandStrategy`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `NONE`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `NONE` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `DEDUPLICATE_DATA_WITH_ZERO` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `DEDUPLICATE_DATA_WITH_ANY_VALUE` (来源: Vector Informatik)
- **描述**: This parameter is used to taylor the CONFIG-CLASS PRE-COMPILE ROM bool data with number deduplication mechanisms. A comparison with 0 is very efficient, but a numerical comparison with a value not 0 can be used to increase the ROM data compression rate.

NONE: ROM data deduplications are switched off.
DEDUPLICATE_DATA_WITH_ZERO: ROM data deduplications can be applied with the value 0.
DEDUPLICATE_DATA_WITH_ANY_VALUE: ROM data deduplications can be applied with any numerical value.

####### 6. SoAdSocketApi

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `73e78b67-49b4-4ab0-aadf-041657fcc7b9`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `SoAdSocketApi`
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **需要索引**: false

**容器描述**:

This container contains configuration parameters describing socket API dependent parameter.

######## 参数配置

此容器包含 **2** 个参数：

######### BOOLEAN 类型参数

########## 1. SoAdVBsdAdUsed

- **UUID**: `573cfcd6-a690-47b9-b16a-c3bad3d20592`
- **参数名**: `SoAdVBsdAdUsed`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter defines if vBsdAd component is used as lower layer.

Note: When vBsdAd component is used instead of MICROSAR TcpIp the following features are not supported:
- Enabling/disabling of UDP checksum (SoAdSocketUdpChecksumEnabled)
- DHCP event callbacks (SoAdDhcpEvent)
- TLS (SoAdSocketTcpTlsConnectionRef)
- Immediate UDP IF Tx Confirmation (SoAdSocketUdpImmediateIfTxConfirmation)
- UDP address resolution retry (SoAdSocketUdpAddressResolutionRetryEnabled)

######### FUNCTION-NAME 类型参数

########## 1. SoAdSocketApiPrefix

- **UUID**: `3886efd8-357d-4d64-b48f-4f50b358ef12`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSocketApiPrefix`
- **数据类型**: FUNCTION-NAME
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **函数变体**:
  - 默认值: `TcpIp`
- **描述**: This parameter defines the function name prefix for the socket API.

Examples:
With a prefix set to "TcpIp", SoAd calls the function "TcpIp_Bind" to bind a socket to a local IP address and port.
With a prefix set to "vBsdAd", SoAd calls the function "vBsdAd_Close" to close a socket.

####### 8. SoAdSecurityEventRefs

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `5ea55763-3936-4d3a-8cb5-02c4f6a44d4b`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `SoAdSecurityEventRefs`
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **需要索引**: false

**容器描述**:

Container for the references to IdsMEvent elements representing the security events that the SoAd module shall report to the IdsM in case the corresponding security related event occurs (and if SoAdEnableSecurityEventReporting is set to "true").

######## 引用关系

此容器包含 **5** 个引用：

######### SYMBOLIC-NAME-REFERENCE 类型引用

########## 1. SOAD_SEV_DROP_MSG_RX_UDP_LENGTH

- **UUID**: `72611749-a2df-4a42-b12f-a389e5366964`
- **引用名**: `SOAD_SEV_DROP_MSG_RX_UDP_LENGTH`
- **引用类型**: SYMBOLIC-NAME-REFERENCE
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **目标**: `IdsMEvent`
- **完整路径**: `/AUTOSAR/EcucDefs/IdsM/IdsMConfiguration/IdsMEvent`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: Reference to IdsMEvent which is reported when SoAd dropped a message received via a UDP socket which contains at least one PDU which violates stack configuration. The violation relates to the length of the PDUs compared to the overall length of the message.

########## 2. SOAD_SEV_DROP_MSG_RX_UDP_SOCKET

- **UUID**: `7680441f-6653-4ed5-9aa2-84838dff360c`
- **引用名**: `SOAD_SEV_DROP_MSG_RX_UDP_SOCKET`
- **引用类型**: SYMBOLIC-NAME-REFERENCE
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **目标**: `IdsMEvent`
- **完整路径**: `/AUTOSAR/EcucDefs/IdsM/IdsMConfiguration/IdsMEvent`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: Reference to IdsMEvent which is reported when SoAd dropped a message received via a UDP socket which violates stack configuration because no suitable socket connection matching to configuration was found.

########## 3. SOAD_SEV_DROP_PDU_RX_TCP

- **UUID**: `deca0dd4-913e-4e80-8168-2c598d977500`
- **引用名**: `SOAD_SEV_DROP_PDU_RX_TCP`

##### 26. SoAdVerifyRxPduCallback

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `0c568c61-d9b7-49a6-9cd1-085e5aef3fb3`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `SoAdVerifyRxPduCallback`
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **需要索引**: false

**容器描述**:

This container contains a callback to verify if a Rx PDU is allowed to be received.

The callback forwards local socket address, remote socket address, PDU Header ID and a configured number of PDU data to an user. If callback fails SoAd drops the received PDU completely. Otherwise SoAd forwards the received PDU.

Via SoAdVerifyRxPduEnable the feature can be enabled on each socket connection separately.

Hint: This feature is only available for TP-PDUs with PDU header option on TCP socket connections.

###### 参数配置

此容器包含 **2** 个参数：

####### INTEGER 类型参数

######## 1. SoAdVerifyRxPduMaxDataLength

- **UUID**: `c242a216-201c-4ff9-afba-c6fe850ac1ef`
- **参数名**: `SoAdVerifyRxPduMaxDataLength`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `0`
- **取值范围**: [0..255]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter defines the number of received PDU data which are forwarded to the user for Rx PDU verification.

####### FUNCTION-NAME 类型参数

######## 1. SoAdVerifyRxPdu

- **UUID**: `34ae0de4-d8e4-4af3-bb09-5e931f747cbd`
- **参数名**: `SoAdVerifyRxPdu`
- **数据类型**: FUNCTION-NAME
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **函数变体**:
- **描述**: This parameter defines the callback name for the Rx PDU verification.

**容器描述**:

Contains the generation configuration parameters of the module SoAd.

###### 参数配置

此容器包含 **16** 个参数：

####### INTEGER 类型参数

######## 1. SoAdReduceBoolDataByNegationThreshold

- **UUID**: `cc4b1ce4-299b-4084-ae56-a5635c178643`
- **参数名**: `SoAdReduceBoolDataByNegationThreshold`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `0`
- **取值范围**: [0..9223372036854775807]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter activates/deactivates the capability to compress boolean CONFIG-CLASS PRE-COMPILE ROM data by using the negation operator.

0: The optimization is deactivated.
>0: If the number of deduplicateable elements is greater than this threshold the data optimization can be performed.

######## 2. SoAdReduceBoolDataByNumericalComparisonThreshold

- **UUID**: `5b7a70fa-f91c-4528-a348-ab9475f1dbc2`
- **参数名**: `SoAdReduceBoolDataByNumericalComparisonThreshold`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `0`
- **取值范围**: [0..9223372036854775807]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter activates/deactivates the capability to compress boolean CONFIG-CLASS PRE-COMPILE ROM data by using numerical comparison with other ROM data.

0: The optimization is deactivated.
>0: If the number of deduplicateable elements is greater than this threshold the data optimization can be performed.

######## 3. SoAdReduceBoolDataByNumericalRelationThreshold

- **UUID**: `f5c326c3-64bb-426c-9bd8-9eb6515548d2`
- **参数名**: `SoAdReduceBoolDataByNumericalRelationThreshold`

- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter activates/deactivates the capability to compress boolean CONFIG-CLASS PRE-COMPILE ROM data by using relational comparison with other ROM data.

0: The optimization is deactivated.
>0: If the number of deduplicateable elements is greater than this threshold the data optimization can be performed.

######## 4. SoAdReduceNumericalDataByOffsetThreshold

- **UUID**: `b116a9c4-d331-4c00-bd88-fca058014897`
- **参数名**: `SoAdReduceNumericalDataByOffsetThreshold`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]

- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false

- **描述**: This parameter activates/deactivates the capability to compress numerical CONFIG-CLASS PRE-COMPILE ROM data by using a constant offset.

0: The optimization is deactivated.
>0: If the number of deduplicateable elements is greater than this threshold the data optimization can be performed.

######## 5. SoAdReduceNumericalDataByArraySubtractionThreshold

- **UUID**: `7f9ff504-5afa-481f-aaf2-405d5133b960`
- **参数名**: `SoAdReduceNumericalDataByArraySubtractionThreshold`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]

- **取值范围**: [0..9223372036854775807]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter activates/deactivates the capability to compress numerical CONFIG-CLASS PRE-COMPILE ROM data by using a subtraction with other ROM data.

0: The optimization is deactivated.
>0: If the number of deduplicateable elements is greater than this threshold the data optimization can be performed.

####### BOOLEAN 类型参数

######## 1. SoAdOutOfBoundsWriteSanitizer

- **UUID**: `7860e17e-d471-4192-978e-5a273d1ad75b`
- **参数名**: `SoAdOutOfBoundsWriteSanitizer`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter activates/deactivates the generation of runtime checks which call a DET error notification function to find easily out of bounds write problems.

FALSE:  no checks are generated in the data access.
TRUE: the data access is enriched with DET checks to validate indexes.

######## 2. SoAdOutOfBoundsReadSanitizer

- **UUID**: `736f51ca-ccf5-420d-8398-f9d2590bae8a`
- **参数名**: `SoAdOutOfBoundsReadSanitizer`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter activates/deactivates the generation of runtime checks which call a DET error notification function to find easily out of bounds read problems.

FALSE:  no checks are generated in the data access.
TRUE: the data access is enriched with DET checks to validate indexes.

######## 3. SoAdReduceConstantData2Define

- **UUID**: `6179eb43-3818-4bab-82c2-e3cedd1ed1ce`
- **参数名**: `SoAdReduceConstantData2Define`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter activates/deactivates the capability to generate CONFIG-CLASS PRE-COMPILE ROM arrays as constant define.

FALSE: ROM arrays are generated as data even if all values are identical.
TRUE: ROM arrays are generated as constant define if all values are identical.

######## 4. SoAdDeduplicateIndirectedData

- **UUID**: `f82c2094-7716-4295-bd80-8e72364fb4df`
- **参数名**: `SoAdDeduplicateIndirectedData`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter activates/deactivates the capability to compress 0:N relational ROM data in all CONFIG-CLASSES without increasing the runtime.

FALSE: 0:N relational ROM data is not compressed.
TRUE: 0:N relational ROM data is compressed without increasing the runtime.

######## 5. SoAdShortSymbols

- **UUID**: `f2bb9304-4cfd-4588-9b99-fe5cfdce1399`
- **参数名**: `SoAdShortSymbols`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter activates/deactivates the capability to generate shortened symbol names.

FALSE: symbol names are generated in a human readable style based on the MIP, tags and variant names.
TRUE: symbol names are generated based on the MIP and a CRC32.

######## 6. SoAdInterfacesForDeactivatedData

- **UUID**: `e209f3f4-afc2-46cc-92de-d4ee97e5399d`
- **参数名**: `SoAdInterfacesForDeactivatedData`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter activates/deactivates the capability to generate bsw data interfaces for deactivated data elements. This is an advantage for the BSW developer to reduce the time to market with a development environment using auto completition and to investigate potential interfaces.

FALSE: data interfaces are not generated if the data elementis deactivated.
TRUE: data interfaces are generated as e.g. empty macros.

######## 7. SoAdReferringKeysInComments

- **UUID**: `c32a1835-1f3c-4cbf-a510-1a54c4912438`
- **参数名**: `SoAdReferringKeysInComments`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter activates/deactivates the capability to generate referring keys in comments. This is an advantage for the developer to investigate indirections, but this feature reduces the overall readability of the generated data.

FALSE: referring keys are not generated in comments.
TRUE: referring keys are generated in comments.

####### ENUMERATION 类型参数

######## 1. SoAdOutOfBoundsWriteProtectionStrategy

- **UUID**: `d40a0d57-2209-48af-9e31-b5d79d4ef04b`
- **参数名**: `SoAdOutOfBoundsWriteProtectionStrategy`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `NONE`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `NONE` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `INDEX_SATURATION` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `INDEX_CHECKING` (来源: Vector Informatik)
- **描述**: This parameter is used to configure a strategy to protect the code to write out of bounds.

NONE: no protection strategy is generated in the data access.
INDEX_SATURATION: arrays are blown up and the data access index is saturated by an appropriate mask. The advantage is the speed of the data access, but own data elements at other indexes of the same variable can be overridden.
INDEX_CHECKING: the data access index is validated by a runtime check. The advantage is that values are never written to incorrect indexes of the data access.

######## 2. SoAdBoolDataInArrayOfStructStrategy

- **UUID**: `01df6817-6e77-41eb-bdd1-725c395dbe8d`
- **参数名**: `SoAdBoolDataInArrayOfStructStrategy`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `BOOLEAN`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `BOOLEAN` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `BITFIELD` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `BITMASKING` (来源: Vector Informatik)
- **描述**: This parameter is used to tailor the usage of boolean data in structures in all CONFIG-CLASSES ROM data. The difference between BITFIELD and BITMASKING depends on your compiler options and memory mapping.

BOOLEAN: the datatype of boolean data is native boolean.
BITFIELD: the bitfield type is used and the compiler extracts the boolean data from structures.
BITMASKING: generated masks are used to extract the boolean data from structures.

######## 3. SoAdDataDeduplicationStrategy

- **UUID**: `8b6a9646-0482-4399-b545-44e13984665d`
- **参数名**: `SoAdDataDeduplicationStrategy`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `NONE`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `NONE` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `DEDUPLICATE_CONST_DATA_WITHOUT_CAST` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `DEDUPLICATE_CONST_DATA_WITH_CAST` (来源: Vector Informatik)
- **描述**: This parameter is used to taylor the the deduplication of CONFIG-CLASS PRE-COMPILE  ROM data.

NONE: The generated data is not deduplicated.
DEDUPLICATE_CONST_DATA_WITHOUT_CAST: The data is deduplicated without using casts.
DEDUPLICATE_CONST_DATA_WITH_CAST: The data is deduplicated using casts.

######## 4. SoAdReduceBoolDataByNumericalOperandStrategy

- **UUID**: `1892f15e-cddf-4bb4-b8f5-9775cb49e99a`
- **参数名**: `SoAdReduceBoolDataByNumericalOperandStrategy`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `NONE`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `NONE` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `DEDUPLICATE_DATA_WITH_ZERO` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `DEDUPLICATE_DATA_WITH_ANY_VALUE` (来源: Vector Informatik)
- **描述**: This parameter is used to taylor the CONFIG-CLASS PRE-COMPILE ROM bool data with number deduplication mechanisms. A comparison with 0 is very efficient, but a numerical comparison with a value not 0 can be used to increase the ROM data compression rate.

NONE: ROM data deduplications are switched off.
DEDUPLICATE_DATA_WITH_ZERO: ROM data deduplications can be applied with the value 0.
DEDUPLICATE_DATA_WITH_ANY_VALUE: ROM data deduplications can be applied with any numerical value.

##### 28. SoAdSocketApi

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `73e78b67-49b4-4ab0-aadf-041657fcc7b9`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `SoAdSocketApi`
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **需要索引**: false

**容器描述**:

This container contains configuration parameters describing socket API dependent parameter.

###### 参数配置

此容器包含 **2** 个参数：

####### BOOLEAN 类型参数

######## 1. SoAdVBsdAdUsed

- **UUID**: `573cfcd6-a690-47b9-b16a-c3bad3d20592`
- **参数名**: `SoAdVBsdAdUsed`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter defines if vBsdAd component is used as lower layer.

Note: When vBsdAd component is used instead of MICROSAR TcpIp the following features are not supported:
- Enabling/disabling of UDP checksum (SoAdSocketUdpChecksumEnabled)
- DHCP event callbacks (SoAdDhcpEvent)
- TLS (SoAdSocketTcpTlsConnectionRef)
- Immediate UDP IF Tx Confirmation (SoAdSocketUdpImmediateIfTxConfirmation)
- UDP address resolution retry (SoAdSocketUdpAddressResolutionRetryEnabled)

####### FUNCTION-NAME 类型参数

######## 1. SoAdSocketApiPrefix

- **UUID**: `3886efd8-357d-4d64-b48f-4f50b358ef12`
- **参数名**: `SoAdSocketApiPrefix`
- **数据类型**: FUNCTION-NAME
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **函数变体**:
  - 默认值: `TcpIp`
- **描述**: This parameter defines the function name prefix for the socket API.

Examples:
With a prefix set to "TcpIp", SoAd calls the function "TcpIp_Bind" to bind a socket to a local IP address and port.
With a prefix set to "vBsdAd", SoAd calls the function "vBsdAd_Close" to close a socket.

- **引用类型**: SYMBOLIC-NAME-REFERENCE
